def test_vulnerable_path_queries_in_uri():
    """Расширение с ?query — тоже должно ловиться."""
    from paradox.detectors.vulnerable_path import VulnerablePathDetector
    def test_typosquatting_sld_only():
        # googleads — поддомен doubleclick, не бренд
        detector = TyposquattingDetector()
        state = _make_state_with_dns(["googleads.g.doubleclick.net"])
        findings = detector.run(state, Config())
        assert not [f for f in findings if f.value == "googleads.g.doubleclick.net"]

    def test_typosquatting_app_subdomain_not_squat():
        # app.loadimpact.com — SLD loadimpact, не apple
        ...

    def test_typosquatting_still_catches_real():
        # micr0soft.com -> SLD micr0soft, levenshtein 1 до microsoft
        ...

    def test_dga_stackoverflow_not_flagged():
        detector = DGADetector()
        state = _make_state_with_dns(["stackoverflow.com"])
        findings = detector.run(state, Config())
        assert not findings

    def test_typosquatting_sld_only_doubleclick():
        """googleads.g.doubleclick.net — SLD doubleclick, не google."""
        detector = TyposquattingDetector()
        state = _make_state_with_dns(["googleads.g.doubleclick.net"])
        findings = detector.run(state, Config())
        assert not [f for f in findings if f.value == "googleads.g.doubleclick.net"]

    def test_typosquatting_app_subdomain_not_squat():
        """app.loadimpact.com — SLD loadimpact, не apple."""
        detector = TyposquattingDetector()
        state = _make_state_with_dns(["app.loadimpact.com"])
        findings = detector.run(state, Config())
        assert not findings

    def test_typosquatting_google_analytics_allowlisted():
        detector = TyposquattingDetector()
        state = _make_state_with_dns(["google-analytics.com"])
        findings = detector.run(state, Config())
        assert not findings

    def test_dga_stackoverflow_not_flagged():
        detector = DGADetector()
        state = _make_state_with_dns(["stackoverflow.com"])
        findings = detector.run(state, Config())
        assert not findings

    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="10.0.1.50", dst_ip="8.8.8.8",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=80,
    )
    fs = FlowStats(packets=1, bytes_sent=500)
    fs.http_uri = "/script.ps1?param=1"
    fs.http_host = "example.com"
    state.flows[key] = fs

    findings = VulnerablePathDetector().run(state, Config())

    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "script_download"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_dhcp.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты DHCP-экстрактора и парсера."""
import struct

from paradox.extractors.dhcp import DHCPExtractor, parse_dhcp
from paradox.models import CaptureState


_MAGIC = b"\x63\x82\x53\x63"


def _build_dhcp(
    op: int = 2,
    xid: int = 0x12345678,
    ciaddr: str = "0.0.0.0",
    yiaddr: str = "192.168.1.50",
    chaddr_mac: str = "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff",
    hostname: str | None = None,
    msg_type: int | None = 5,
) -> bytes:
    """Собрать минимальный DHCP-пакет."""
    def ip_bytes(s):
        return bytes(int(x) for x in s.split("."))

    mac_bytes = bytes(int(x, 16) for x in chaddr_mac.split(":"))
    chaddr = mac_bytes + b"\x00" * (16 - len(mac_bytes))

    pkt = bytearray()
    pkt.append(op)          # op
    pkt.append(1)           # htype = Ethernet
    pkt.append(6)           # hlen = 6
    pkt.append(0)           # hops
    pkt += struct.pack(">I", xid)   # xid
    pkt += b"\x00\x00"    # secs
    pkt += b"\x00\x00"    # flags
    pkt += ip_bytes(ciaddr)
    pkt += ip_bytes(yiaddr)
    pkt += ip_bytes("0.0.0.0")      # siaddr
    pkt += ip_bytes("0.0.0.0")      # giaddr
    pkt += chaddr                   # 16 bytes
    pkt += b"\x00" * 64            # sname
    pkt += b"\x00" * 128           # file
    pkt += _MAGIC

    # Options
    if msg_type is not None:
        pkt.append(53)
        pkt.append(1)
        pkt.append(msg_type)
    if hostname:
        hn = hostname.encode("ascii")
        pkt.append(12)
        pkt.append(len(hn))
        pkt += hn
    pkt.append(255)  # END

    # Padding до минимальной длины 240+ (уже больше, но на всякий)
    while len(pkt) < 240:
        pkt.append(0)

    return bytes(pkt)


class _Rec:
    """Заглушка PacketRecord для тестов."""
    def __init__(self, payload, src_port=68, dst_port=67):
        self.payload = payload
        self.src_port = src_port
        self.dst_port = dst_port
        self.src_ip = "0.0.0.0"
        self.dst_ip = "255.255.255.255"


# ---------- parse_dhcp ----------

def test_parse_minimal_ack():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(yiaddr="192.168.1.50", msg_type=5)
    info = parse_dhcp(pkt)
    assert info is not None
    assert info["op"] == 2
    assert info["yiaddr"] == "192.168.1.50"
    assert info["chaddr"] == "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff"
    assert info["msg_type"] == 5


def test_parse_with_hostname():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(hostname="laptop-01")
    info = parse_dhcp(pkt)
    assert info["hostname"] == "laptop-01"


def test_parse_too_short():
    assert parse_dhcp(b"\x00" * 100) is None


def test_parse_no_magic():
    pkt = b"\x00" * 240
    assert parse_dhcp(pkt) is None


def test_parse_no_msg_type():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(msg_type=None)
    info = parse_dhcp(pkt)
    assert info["msg_type"] is None


def test_parse_zero_mac():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(chaddr_mac="00:00:00:00:00:00")
    info = parse_dhcp(pkt)
    assert info["chaddr"] == ""


# ---------- DHCPExtractor ----------

def test_extractor_writes_identity():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(
        yiaddr="192.168.1.50",
        chaddr_mac="aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff",
        hostname="laptop-01",
    )
    state = CaptureState()
    DHCPExtractor().process(_Rec(pkt), state)

    ident = state.host_identities.get("192.168.1.50")
    assert ident is not None
    assert ident.mac == "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff"
    assert "laptop-01" in ident.hostnames


def test_extractor_ignores_non_dhcp_port():
    pkt = _build_dhcp()
    state = CaptureState()
    DHCPExtractor().process(_Rec(pkt, src_port=53, dst_port=53), state)
    assert not state.host_identities


def test_extractor_ignores_empty_payload():
    state = CaptureState()
    DHCPExtractor().process(_Rec(b""), state)
    assert not state.host_identities


def test_extractor_uses_ciaddr_when_yiaddr_zero():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(
        ciaddr="10.0.0.5",
        yiaddr="0.0.0.0",
        chaddr_mac="11:22:33:44:55:66",
    )
    state = CaptureState()
    DHCPExtractor().process(_Rec(pkt), state)
    ident = state.host_identities.get("10.0.0.5")
    assert ident is not None
    assert ident.mac == "11:22:33:44:55:66"


def test_extractor_ignores_both_zero():
    pkt = _build_dhcp(ciaddr="0.0.0.0", yiaddr="0.0.0.0")
    state = CaptureState()
    DHCPExtractor().process(_Rec(pkt), state)
    assert not state.host_identities


def test_extractor_merges_with_existing_identity():
    """Если IP уже в host_identities (например, из ARP) — дополняем."""
    from paradox.models import HostIdentity
    state = CaptureState()
    state.host_identities["192.168.1.50"] = HostIdentity(
        ip="192.168.1.50", mac="11:11:11:11:11:11",
    )
    pkt = _build_dhcp(
        yiaddr="192.168.1.50",
        chaddr_mac="aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff",
        hostname="new-host",
    )
    DHCPExtractor().process(_Rec(pkt), state)

    ident = state.host_identities["192.168.1.50"]
    # DHCP перетирает MAC (более авторитетный источник)
    assert ident.mac == "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff"
    assert "new-host" in ident.hostnames


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_direct_ip_no_dns.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты правила direct_ip_no_dns (hardcoded IP / прямой C2)."""
from paradox.contradictions.base import Fact
from paradox.contradictions.rules.g_direct_ip_no_dns import (
    DirectIpNoDnsRule,
)


def _connect(src, dst, ports=None, bytes_sent=5000, resolved=False):
    return Fact(
        source="flow",
        subject=src,
        predicate="direct_ip_connect",
        value=dst,
        ts=0.0,
        confidence=0.9,
        evidence={
            "dst_ip": dst,
            "ports": ports or [443],
            "bytes_sent": bytes_sent,
            "resolved": resolved,
        },
    )


def _dns(src, count=5):
    return Fact(
        source="dns",
        subject=src,
        predicate="makes_dns_queries",
        value="",
        ts=0.0,
        confidence=0.9,
        evidence={"count": count, "sample": ["example.com"]},
    )


# 1. 3+ dst, DNS нет → medium
def test_three_dsts_no_dns_medium():
    facts = [
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4"),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "5.6.7.8"),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "9.10.11.12"),
    ]
    rule = DirectIpNoDnsRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].severity == "medium"
    assert res[0].subject == "192.168.1.5"
    assert "DNS-запросов — ноль" in res[0].resolution_hint


# 2. 1 dst, 10+ ports → срабатывает (много обращений к одному IP)
def test_one_dst_many_ports():
    facts = [
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4",
                 ports=list(range(1000, 1010))),
    ]
    rule = DirectIpNoDnsRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1


# 3. Все dst резолвились через DNS → не срабатывает.
# Новая логика: смотрим per-IP resolved, не общий факт «был DNS».
def test_all_resolved_no_contradiction():
    facts = [
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4", resolved=True),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "5.6.7.8", resolved=True),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "9.10.11.12", resolved=True),
    ]
    rule = DirectIpNoDnsRule()
    assert rule.find_contradictions(facts) == []


# 3b. Смешанные: часть resolved, часть нет → срабатывает по unresolved.
# resolved-факты отфильтровываются, но остальных хватает для threshold.
def test_mixed_resolved_triggers():
    facts = [
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4", resolved=True),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "5.6.7.8", resolved=False),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "9.10.11.12", resolved=False),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "13.14.15.16", resolved=False),
    ]
    rule = DirectIpNoDnsRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].subject == "192.168.1.5"
    # В facts — только unresolved (после фильтра)
    assert all(
        not f.evidence.get("resolved", False)
        for f in res[0].facts
    )


# 4. Один dst, один порт → шум, не срабатывает
def test_single_connect_no_contradiction():
    facts = [_connect("192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4", ports=[443])]
    rule = DirectIpNoDnsRule()
    assert rule.find_contradictions(facts) == []


# 5. Разные клиенты изолированы
def test_different_clients_isolated():
    facts = [
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4"),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "5.6.7.8"),
        _connect("192.168.1.5", "9.10.11.12"),
        _dns("192.168.1.6"),  # чужой клиент, на 1.5 не влияет
    ]
    rule = DirectIpNoDnsRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].subject == "192.168.1.5"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_dns_answer_mismatch.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты правила dns_answer_mismatch."""
from paradox.contradictions.base import Fact
from paradox.contradictions.rules.h_dns_answer_mismatch import (
    DnsAnswerMismatchRule,
)


def _sni(src, value, dst_ip, resolved=True, resolved_ips=None):
    return Fact(
        source="sni",
        subject=src,
        predicate="sni",
        value=value,
        ts=0.0,
        confidence=0.9,
        evidence={
            "dst_ip": dst_ip,
            "dst_port": 443,
            "src_port": 50000,
            "resolved": resolved,
            "resolved_ips": resolved_ips or [],
        },
    )


def test_mismatch_triggers():
    facts = [_sni("192.168.1.5", "evil.example", "1.2.3.4",
                  resolved_ips=["5.6.7.8"])]
    rule = DnsAnswerMismatchRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].subject == "192.168.1.5"
    assert res[0].severity == "medium"
    assert "evil.example" in res[0].resolution_hint


def test_match_no_contradiction():
    facts = [_sni("192.168.1.5", "ok.example", "5.6.7.8",
                  resolved_ips=["5.6.7.8", "9.10.11.12"])]
    rule = DnsAnswerMismatchRule()
    assert rule.find_contradictions(facts) == []


def test_not_resolved_ignored():
    # resolved=False → это sni_without_dns, не наше правило.
    facts = [_sni("192.168.1.5", "evil.example", "1.2.3.4", resolved=False)]
    rule = DnsAnswerMismatchRule()
    assert rule.find_contradictions(facts) == []


def test_no_resolved_ips_ignored():
    # resolved=True, но resolved_ips пуст → нет терминального A.
    facts = [_sni("192.168.1.5", "cname.example", "1.2.3.4",
                  resolved=True, resolved_ips=[])]
    rule = DnsAnswerMismatchRule()
    assert rule.find_contradictions(facts) == []


def test_mixed_same_sni_dedup():
    # Один SNI, два flow: один на правильный IP, другой на левый.
    facts = [
        _sni("192.168.1.5", "ok.example", "5.6.7.8",
             resolved_ips=["5.6.7.8"]),
        _sni("192.168.1.5", "ok.example", "9.9.9.9",
             resolved_ips=["5.6.7.8"]),
    ]
    rule = DnsAnswerMismatchRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert len(res[0].facts) == 1  # только один mismatched dst_ip
    assert res[0].facts[0].evidence["dst_ip"] == "9.9.9.9"


def test_different_clients_isolated():
    facts = [
        _sni("192.168.1.5", "evil.example", "1.2.3.4",
             resolved_ips=["5.6.7.8"]),
        _sni("192.168.1.6", "ok.example", "5.6.7.8",
             resolved_ips=["5.6.7.8"]),
    ]
    rule = DnsAnswerMismatchRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    assert res[0].subject == "192.168.1.5"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_dns_tunneling.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты DNSTunnelingDetector.

Проверяем:
- длинные уникальные qname под одним base → finding
- короткие qname (DGA) → не finding
- обычные домены → не finding
- мало запросов → не finding
- allowlist (DoH) → не finding

Важно:
- qname в тестах длиннее dns_tunneling_min_qname_length (50).
- total_queries = sum(counts), порог dns_tunneling_min_queries (50).
  В тестах ставим count=2 на qname, чтобы 30 unique давали 60 total.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.detectors.dns_tunneling import DNSTunnelingDetector
from paradox.models import CaptureState


# 35-символьный префикс. С "000000.dns.attacker.com" даёт ~58 символов —
# уверенно выше порога 50.
_LONG_PREFIX = "aGVsbG8gd29ybGQgaGVsbG8gd29ybGQK000"

# Каждый qname повторяется по 2 раза, чтобы total_queries >= 50.
# В реальном DNS-туннеле запросов будет 1000+, здесь — минимально достаточное.
_QUERY_COUNT = 2


def _state(src_ip, qnames):
    """qnames: dict[qname -> count]."""
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries_by_src[src_ip] = dict(qnames)
    return state


def test_dns_tunneling_detects_long_unique_subdomains():
    """30 длинных уникальных qname под attacker.com — finding."""
    qnames = {
        f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.attacker.com": _QUERY_COUNT
        for i in range(30)
    }
    state = _state("192.168.1.100", qnames)
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "dns_tunneling"
    assert findings[0].evidence["base_domain"] == "attacker.com"
    assert findings[0].evidence["long_qnames_count"] >= 20
    assert findings[0].confidence >= 0.7


def test_dns_tunneling_ignores_short_dga():
    """DGA-поддомены короткие — не DNS tunneling."""
    qnames = {
        f"dackrpbfnmwjl{i}.pwned.se": _QUERY_COUNT
        for i in range(50)
    }
    state = _state("192.168.1.100", qnames)
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_dns_tunneling_ignores_normal():
    """Обычные домены — не tunneling."""
    qnames = {
        "www.google.com": 50,
        "mail.google.com": 30,
        "docs.google.com": 10,
    }
    state = _state("192.168.1.100", qnames)
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_dns_tunneling_below_min_queries():
    """5 запросов — меньше порога 50."""
    qnames = {
        f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.attacker.com": 1
        for i in range(5)
    }
    state = _state("192.168.1.100", qnames)
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_dns_tunneling_below_min_long_qnames():
    """50 запросов, но только 3 длинных — не tunneling."""
    qnames = {
        "short.attacker.com": 47,
    }
    for i in range(3):
        qnames[f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.attacker.com"] = 1
    state = _state("192.168.1.100", qnames)
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_dns_tunneling_ignores_allowlist_doh():
    """DoH-сервисы не считаются tunneling."""
    qnames = {
        f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.google": _QUERY_COUNT
        for i in range(30)
    }
    state = _state("192.168.1.100", qnames)
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_dns_tunneling_separate_srcs():
    """Разные src с разными base — разные findings."""
    state = CaptureState()
    state.dns_queries_by_src["192.168.1.100"] = {
        f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.attacker.com": _QUERY_COUNT
        for i in range(30)
    }
    state.dns_queries_by_src["192.168.1.101"] = {
        f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.evil.org": _QUERY_COUNT
        for i in range(30)
    }
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 2
    values = sorted(f.value for f in findings)
    assert values == [
        "192.168.1.100 -> attacker.com",
        "192.168.1.101 -> evil.org",
    ]


def test_dns_tunneling_empty_state():
    """Пустой CaptureState — не падает."""
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(CaptureState(), Config())
    assert findings == []


def test_dns_tunneling_multiple_base_domains_same_src():
    """Один src, два base домена — два finding'а."""
    state = CaptureState()
    qnames = {}
    for i in range(30):
        qnames[f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.attacker.com"] = _QUERY_COUNT
    for i in range(30):
        qnames[f"{_LONG_PREFIX}{i:06d}.dns.evil.org"] = _QUERY_COUNT
    state.dns_queries_by_src["192.168.1.100"] = qnames
    findings = DNSTunnelingDetector().run(state, Config())
    assert len(findings) == 2

# FILE: tests/test_domain_utils.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты domain_utils: split_domain / base_domain / registered_label / matches_suffix."""
from paradox.domain_utils import (
    base_domain,
    matches_suffix,
    registered_label,
    split_domain,
)


# ---------- split_domain ----------

def test_split_simple():
    assert split_domain("example.com") == ("example.com", "")


def test_split_subdomain():
    assert split_domain("api.example.com") == ("example.com", "api")


def test_split_deep_subdomain():
    assert split_domain("a.b.c.example.com") == ("example.com", "a.b.c")


def test_split_two_level_uk():
    assert split_domain("foo.co.uk") == ("foo.co.uk", "")


def test_split_two_level_uk_with_sub():
    assert split_domain("api.foo.co.uk") == ("foo.co.uk", "api")


def test_split_two_level_au():
    assert split_domain("a.b.example.com.au") == ("example.com.au", "a.b")


def test_split_two_level_jp():
    assert split_domain("www.example.co.jp") == ("example.co.jp", "www")


def test_split_two_level_br():
    assert split_domain("x.y.z.com.br") == ("z.com.br", "x.y")


def test_split_single_label():
    assert split_domain("localhost") == ("localhost", "")


def test_split_empty():
    assert split_domain("") == ("", "")


def test_split_trailing_dot():
    assert split_domain("example.com.") == ("example.com", "")


def test_split_case_insensitive():
    assert split_domain("API.Example.COM") == ("example.com", "api")


# ---------- base_domain ----------

def test_base_simple():
    assert base_domain("api.example.com") == "example.com"


def test_base_uk():
    assert base_domain("a.b.foo.co.uk") == "foo.co.uk"


def test_base_no_sub():
    assert base_domain("attacker.com") == "attacker.com"


def test_base_uk_no_sub():
    assert base_domain("foo.co.uk") == "foo.co.uk"


def test_base_different_organisations_uk():
    """foo.co.uk и bar.co.uk — РАЗНЫЕ организации. Ключевой кейс."""
    assert base_domain("foo.co.uk") != base_domain("bar.co.uk")


# ---------- registered_label ----------

def test_label_simple():
    assert registered_label("api.example.com") == "example"


def test_label_uk():
    assert registered_label("a.b.foo.co.uk") == "foo"


def test_label_doubleclick():
    assert (
        registered_label("googleads.g.doubleclick.net") == "doubleclick"
    )


def test_label_no_dot():
    assert registered_label("localhost") == "localhost"


# ---------- matches_suffix ----------

def test_suffix_exact():
    assert matches_suffix("example.com", {"example.com"})


def test_suffix_subdomain():
    assert matches_suffix("api.example.com", {"example.com"})


def test_suffix_no_partial_label():
    """notexample.com НЕ должно матчиться по example.com."""
    assert not matches_suffix("notexample.com", {"example.com"})


def test_suffix_empty_host():
    assert not matches_suffix("", {"example.com"})


def test_suffix_two_level():
    assert matches_suffix("api.foo.co.uk", {"foo.co.uk"})


def test_suffix_two_level_not_sibling():
    assert not matches_suffix("bar.co.uk", {"foo.co.uk"})


def test_suffix_multiple():
    assert matches_suffix("x.google-analytics.com",
                          {"cloudfront.net", "google-analytics.com"})


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_editable_list.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты EditableListWidget."""
import os

os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen")

import pytest


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def qapp():
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    yield app


@pytest.fixture
def widget(qapp):
    from paradox_gui.widgets.editable_list import EditableListWidget
    return EditableListWidget(port_mode=False)


@pytest.fixture
def port_widget(qapp):
    from paradox_gui.widgets.editable_list import EditableListWidget
    return EditableListWidget(port_mode=True)


def test_empty_by_default(widget):
    assert widget.items() == []
    assert widget.toPlainText() == ""


def test_setplaintext_parses_newlines(widget):
    widget.setPlainText("a\nb\nc")
    assert widget.items() == ["a", "b", "c"]


def test_setplaintext_parses_commas(widget):
    widget.setPlainText("a, b, c")
    assert widget.items() == ["a", "b", "c"]


def test_setplaintext_dedups(widget):
    widget.setPlainText("a\na\nb")
    assert widget.items() == ["a", "b"]


def test_toplaintext_roundtrip(widget):
    widget.setPlainText("x\ny\nz")
    assert widget.toPlainText() == "x\ny\nz"


def test_add_valid_value(widget):
    widget._input.setText("hello")
    widget._add_from_input()
    assert widget.items() == ["hello"]
    assert widget._input.text() == ""


def test_add_rejects_duplicate(widget):
    widget.setItems(["a"])
    widget._input.setText("a")
    widget._add_from_input()
    assert widget.items() == ["a"]


def test_port_mode_rejects_non_numeric(port_widget):
    port_widget._input.setText("abc")
    port_widget._add_from_input()
    assert port_widget.items() == []


def test_port_mode_rejects_out_of_range(port_widget):
    port_widget._input.setText("99999")
    port_widget._add_from_input()
    assert port_widget.items() == []


def test_port_mode_accepts_valid(port_widget):
    port_widget._input.setText("8080")
    port_widget._add_from_input()
    assert port_widget.items() == ["8080"]


def test_remove_selected(widget):
    widget.setItems(["a", "b", "c"])
    widget._list.setCurrentRow(1)
    widget._remove_selected()
    assert widget.items() == ["a", "c"]


def test_textchanged_emitted(widget):
    got: list[int] = []
    widget.textChanged.connect(lambda: got.append(1))
    widget._input.setText("x")
    widget._add_from_input()
    assert got, "textChanged не сработал"

# FILE: tests/test_editable_list_buttons.py
# ==========================================
"""Дополнительные тесты: кнопки."""
import os

os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen")

import pytest


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def qapp():
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    yield app


@pytest.fixture
def widget(qapp):
    from paradox_gui.widgets.editable_list import EditableListWidget
    return EditableListWidget(port_mode=False)


def test_add_button_has_text(widget):
    assert widget._add_btn.text() == "Добавить"


def test_delete_button_has_text(widget):
    assert "Удалить" in widget._del_btn.text()


def test_delete_button_disabled_without_selection(widget):
    widget.setItems(["a", "b"])
    assert widget._del_btn.isEnabled() is False


def test_delete_button_enabled_on_selection(widget):
    widget.setItems(["a", "b"])
    widget._list.setCurrentRow(0)
    assert widget._del_btn.isEnabled() is True


def test_delete_button_disabled_after_removal(widget):
    widget.setItems(["a"])
    widget._list.setCurrentRow(0)
    widget._remove_selected()
    assert widget._del_btn.isEnabled() is False


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_editable_list_io.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты импорта/экспорта EditableListWidget."""
import os

os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen")

import pytest


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def qapp():
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    yield app


@pytest.fixture
def widget(qapp):
    from paradox_gui.widgets.editable_list import EditableListWidget
    return EditableListWidget(port_mode=False)


@pytest.fixture
def port_widget(qapp):
    from paradox_gui.widgets.editable_list import EditableListWidget
    return EditableListWidget(port_mode=True)


def test_parse_import_basic(widget):
    values, skipped = widget._parse_import_text("a\nb\nc")
    assert values == ["a", "b", "c"]
    assert skipped == 0


def test_parse_import_ignores_comments(widget):
    text = "# header\na\n\n# comment\nb"
    values, _ = widget._parse_import_text(text)
    assert values == ["a", "b"]


def test_parse_import_dedups(widget):
    values, _ = widget._parse_import_text("a\na\nb\nb")
    assert values == ["a", "b"]


def test_parse_import_csv_style(widget):
    values, _ = widget._parse_import_text("a, b, c")
    assert values == ["a", "b", "c"]


def test_parse_import_port_mode_filters_invalid(port_widget):
    values, skipped = port_widget._parse_import_text(
        "80\nabc\n443\n99999\n8080"
    )
    assert values == ["80", "443", "8080"]
    assert skipped == 2


def test_import_buttons_exist(widget):
    assert hasattr(widget, "_import_btn")
    assert hasattr(widget, "_export_btn")
    assert "Загрузить" in widget._import_btn.text()
    assert "Экспорт" in widget._export_btn.text()


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_enrich_config.py
# ==========================================
"""Config.enrichment_enabled_providers содержит 9 провайдеров.

6 безключевых + 3 abuse.ch (требуют ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY, но видны
в списке «Настройки → Threat Intel»).
"""
from paradox.config import Config


def test_default_providers_count():
    cfg = Config()
    assert len(cfg.enrichment_enabled_providers) == 9


def test_keyless_providers_enabled_by_default():
    cfg = Config()
    for p in ("shodan_internetdb", "greynoise", "alienvault_otx", "urlscan"):
        assert p in cfg.enrichment_enabled_providers


def test_keyed_providers_in_default():
    """abuseipdb/virustotal требуют ключ, но в списке."""
    cfg = Config()
    for p in ("abuseipdb", "virustotal"):
        assert p in cfg.enrichment_enabled_providers


def test_abusеch_providers_in_default():
    """abuse.ch-провайдеры в списке — но без ключа BaseEnricher.enabled
    возвращает False, поэтому запросы не идут."""
    cfg = Config()
    for p in ("urlhaus", "malwarebazaar", "threatfox"):
        assert p in cfg.enrichment_enabled_providers


def test_all_registry_names_present_in_default():
    """В Config должны быть все имена из REGISTRY — иначе провайдер
    никогда не вызовется."""
    from paradox.integrations.registry import REGISTRY
    cfg = Config()
    missing = set(REGISTRY) - set(cfg.enrichment_enabled_providers)
    assert not missing, f"нет в Config: {sorted(missing)}"

# FILE: tests/test_enrich_greynoise.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты GreyNoiseEnricher."""
from paradox.integrations.greynoise import GreyNoiseEnricher


def test_supports_only_ip():
    assert GreyNoiseEnricher().supports == ("ip",)


def test_no_key_required():
    e = GreyNoiseEnricher()
    assert e.enabled


def test_lookup_domain_rejected():
    e = GreyNoiseEnricher()
    r = e.lookup("evil.com", "domain")
    assert not r.ok
    assert "unsupported" in r.error


def test_lookup_success(monkeypatch):
    e = GreyNoiseEnricher()
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (
        {"noise": True, "riot": False, "classification": "malicious"}, None,
    ))
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data["noise"] is True


def test_lookup_not_found(monkeypatch):
    e = GreyNoiseEnricher()
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (None, None))
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data == {}


def test_lookup_error(monkeypatch):
    e = GreyNoiseEnricher()
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (None, "rate limited (429)"))
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert not r.ok

# ==========================================
"""Тесты MalwareBazaarEnricher."""
from paradox.integrations.malwarebazaar import MalwareBazaarEnricher


def test_disabled_without_key(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY", raising=False)
    e = MalwareBazaarEnricher(auth_key=None)
    assert not e.enabled


def test_supports_hash_only():
    assert MalwareBazaarEnricher(auth_key="x").supports == ("hash",)


def test_lookup_ip_unsupported():
    e = MalwareBazaarEnricher(auth_key="x")
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert not r.ok
    assert "unsupported" in r.error


def test_lookup_success(monkeypatch):
    e = MalwareBazaarEnricher(auth_key="x")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (
        {"query_status": "ok", "data": [{"sha256_hash": i}]}, None,
    ))
    r = e.lookup("a" * 64, "hash")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data["query_status"] == "ok"


def test_lookup_hash_not_found(monkeypatch):
    e = MalwareBazaarEnricher(auth_key="x")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (None, None))
    r = e.lookup("a" * 64, "hash")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data == {}

# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_enrich_pipeline.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты attach_enrichment_to_findings и enrich_findings (без сети).
"""
import time
from dataclasses import replace

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.integrations.base import EnrichmentResult
from paradox.integrations.pipeline import (
    attach_enrichment_to_findings,
)
from paradox.models import Finding


def test_attach_enrichment_to_ip_finding():
    """dst_ip → evidence["enrichment"]["8.8.8.8"]["shodan_internetdb"]."""
    findings = [
        Finding(
            type="suspicious_port", value="8.8.8.8:4444",
            confidence=0.5, evidence={"dst_ip": "8.8.8.8"},
        ),
    ]
    enrichment_map = {
        ("ip", "8.8.8.8"): [
            EnrichmentResult(
                source="shodan_internetdb", ioc="8.8.8.8", ioc_type="ip",
                data={"ports": [53, 443]},
                fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
            ),
        ],
    }

    attach_enrichment_to_findings(findings, enrichment_map)

    ev = findings[0].evidence["enrichment"]
    assert "8.8.8.8" in ev
    assert ev["8.8.8.8"]["shodan_internetdb"]["ports"] == [53, 443]


def test_attach_enrichment_skips_errors():
    """Ошибочный EnrichmentResult не прикрепляется."""
    findings = [
        Finding(type="x", value="8.8.8.8", confidence=0.5,
                evidence={"dst_ip": "8.8.8.8"}),
    ]
    enrichment_map = {
        ("ip", "8.8.8.8"): [
            EnrichmentResult(
                source="shodan_internetdb", ioc="8.8.8.8", ioc_type="ip",
                error="timeout",
                fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
            ),
        ],
    }

    attach_enrichment_to_findings(findings, enrichment_map)

    assert "enrichment" not in findings[0].evidence


def test_attach_enrichment_no_matching_iocs():
    """Если в map ничего нет — evidence не меняется."""
    findings = [
        Finding(type="x", value="1.2.3.4", confidence=0.5,
                evidence={"dst_ip": "1.2.3.4"}),
    ]
    attach_enrichment_to_findings(findings, {})
    assert "enrichment" not in findings[0].evidence


def test_attach_enrichment_multiple_sources():
    """Два провайдера на один IP — оба в evidence."""
    findings = [
        Finding(type="x", value="8.8.8.8", confidence=0.5,
                evidence={"dst_ip": "8.8.8.8"}),
    ]
    enrichment_map = {
        ("ip", "8.8.8.8"): [
            EnrichmentResult(
                source="shodan_internetdb", ioc="8.8.8.8", ioc_type="ip",
                data={"ports": [53]},
                fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
            ),
            EnrichmentResult(
                source="virustotal", ioc="8.8.8.8", ioc_type="ip",
                data={"malicious": 0, "harmless": 70},
                fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
            ),
        ],
    }

    attach_enrichment_to_findings(findings, enrichment_map)

    ev = findings[0].evidence["enrichment"]["8.8.8.8"]
    assert "shodan_internetdb" in ev
    assert "virustotal" in ev

# FILE: tests/test_enrich_registry_full.py
# ==========================================
"""Реестр содержит все 9 провайдеров."""
from paradox.integrations.registry import REGISTRY, list_providers


def test_all_nine_present():
    expected = {
        "shodan_internetdb", "abuseipdb", "virustotal",
        "greynoise", "alienvault_otx", "urlscan",
        "urlhaus", "malwarebazaar", "threatfox",
    }
    assert set(REGISTRY) == expected


def test_registry_names_match_instances():
    for name, cls in REGISTRY.items():
        try:
            inst = cls()
        except TypeError:
            inst = cls(auth_key="")
        assert inst.name == name


def test_list_providers_sorted():
    assert list_providers() == sorted(list_providers())

"""Тесты ThreatFoxEnricher."""
from paradox.integrations.threatfox import ThreatFoxEnricher


def test_disabled_without_key(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY", raising=False)
    e = ThreatFoxEnricher(auth_key=None)
    assert not e.enabled


def test_supports_ip_domain_hash():
    assert set(ThreatFoxEnricher(auth_key="x").supports) == {"ip", "domain", "hash"}


def test_lookup_success(monkeypatch):
    e = ThreatFoxEnricher(auth_key="x")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (
        {"query_status": "ok", "data": [{"ioc": i}]}, None,
    ))
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data["query_status"] == "ok"


def test_lookup_no_result(monkeypatch):
    e = ThreatFoxEnricher(auth_key="x")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (None, None))
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data == {}

"""Тесты URLhausEnricher."""
from paradox.integrations.urlhaus import URLhausEnricher


def test_disabled_without_key(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY", raising=False)
    e = URLhausEnricher(auth_key=None)
    assert not e.enabled
    r = e.lookup("evil.com", "domain")
    assert not r.ok
    assert "ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY" in r.error


def test_enabled_with_key():
    e = URLhausEnricher(auth_key="fake")
    assert e.enabled


def test_supports_domain_url():
    assert set(URLhausEnricher(auth_key="x").supports) == {"domain", "url"}


def test_lookup_success(monkeypatch):
    e = URLhausEnricher(auth_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (
        {"query_status": "ok", "urls": [{"url": "http://evil.com/x"}]}, None,
    ))
    r = e.lookup("evil.com", "domain")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data["query_status"] == "ok"


def test_lookup_no_results_returns_empty(monkeypatch):
    e = URLhausEnricher(auth_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (None, None))
    r = e.lookup("clean.com", "domain")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data == {}

from paradox.integrations.urlscan import URLScanEnricher

    assert set(URLScanEnricher().supports) == {"domain", "url"}

    assert URLScanEnricher().enabled

    e = URLScanEnricher()
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda i, t: (
        {"results": [{"_id": "abc123", "page": {"url": "http://evil.com"}}]}, None,

    r = e.lookup("clean.com", "domain")
    assert r.ok
    assert r.data == {}

    e = URLScanEnricher()
    r = e.lookup("1.2.3.4", "ip")
    assert not r.ok
    assert "unsupported" in r.error


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_enricher_orchestrator.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты orchestrator: enabled, категории ошибок, private IP."""
import ipaddress
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.integrations.base import EnrichmentResult
from paradox.integrations.enricher import (
    EnricherOrchestrator,
    _is_private_ip,
)


# -- private IP ----------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ip", [
    "10.0.0.1", "10.255.255.255",
    "172.16.0.1", "172.31.0.1",
    "192.168.1.1",
    "127.0.0.1", "::1",
    "169.254.1.1", "fe80::1",
    "224.0.0.1", "239.0.0.1",
    "100.64.0.1",           # CGNAT
    "0.0.0.0",
    "fc00::1",              # IPv6 ULA
])
def test_private_ip_true(ip):
    assert _is_private_ip(ip) is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ip", [
    "8.8.8.8", "1.1.1.1", "2001:4860::8888", "2606:4700::1111",
])
def test_public_ip_false(ip):
    assert _is_private_ip(ip) is False


def test_garbage_ip_is_false():
    assert _is_private_ip("not an ip") is False
    assert _is_private_ip("") is False
    assert _is_private_ip(None) is False


# -- orchestrator: enabled=False не дёргается ----------------------------


@dataclass
class FakeProvider:
    name: str
    supports: tuple
    enabled: bool = True
    calls: int = 0

    def lookup(self, ioc, ioc_type):
        self.calls += 1
        return EnrichmentResult(
            source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
            data={"ok": True}, fetched_at=0.0, ttl_days=1,
        )


class FakeCache:
    def __init__(self):
        self.data = {}

    def get(self, source, ioc):
        return self.data.get((source, ioc))

    def put(self, result):
        self.data[(result.source, result.ioc)] = result

    def close(self):
        pass


def test_disabled_provider_not_called():
    p = FakeProvider(name="x", supports=("ip",), enabled=False)
    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([p], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert p.calls == 0
    assert orch.stats["skipped_disabled"] == 1


def test_enabled_provider_called():
    p = FakeProvider(name="shodan_internetdb", supports=("ip",), enabled=True)
    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([p], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert p.calls == 1
    assert orch.stats["provider_calls"] == 1


def test_unsupported_skipped():
    """Провайдер, ВКЛЮЧЁННЫЙ в Config, но не поддерживающий тип —
    попадает в skipped_unsupported (не в skipped_disabled).

    Используем malwarebazaar (включён в Config по умолчанию), запрос
    идёт по ip — провайдер поддерживает только hash.
    """
    p = FakeProvider(name="malwarebazaar", supports=("hash",))
    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([p], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert p.calls == 0
    assert orch.stats["skipped_unsupported"] == 1
    assert orch.stats["skipped_disabled"] == 0


def test_disabled_outside_config_goes_to_disabled_counter():
    """Провайдер, НЕ включённый в Config — skipped_disabled."""
    p = FakeProvider(name="totally_unknown_provider", supports=("ip",))
    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([p], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert p.calls == 0
    assert orch.stats["skipped_disabled"] == 1


def test_stats_has_new_counters():
    p = FakeProvider(name="shodan_internetdb", supports=("ip",))
    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([p], FakeCache(), Config())
    for key in ("errors_network", "errors_auth", "errors_server",
                "skipped_disabled", "skipped_unsupported"):
        assert key in orch.stats


def test_error_categories_network():
    @dataclass
    class P:
        name: str = "greynoise"
        supports: tuple = ("ip",)
        enabled: bool = True

        def lookup(self, ioc, ioc_type):
            return EnrichmentResult(
                source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
                error="URLError: timeout", fetched_at=0.0, ttl_days=1,
            )

    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([P()], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert orch.stats["errors"] == 1
    assert orch.stats["errors_network"] == 1


def test_error_categories_auth():
    @dataclass
    class P:
        name: str = "abuseipdb"
        supports: tuple = ("ip",)
        enabled: bool = True

        def lookup(self, ioc, ioc_type):
            return EnrichmentResult(
                source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
                error="unauthorized (401)", fetched_at=0.0, ttl_days=1,
            )

    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([P()], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert orch.stats["errors_auth"] == 1


def test_error_categories_server():
    @dataclass
    class P:
        name: str = "virustotal"
        supports: tuple = ("ip",)
        enabled: bool = True

        def lookup(self, ioc, ioc_type):
            return EnrichmentResult(
                source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
                error="server error (503)", fetched_at=0.0, ttl_days=1,
            )

    orch = EnricherOrchestrator([P()], FakeCache(), Config())
    orch.enrich([("ip", "8.8.8.8")])
    assert orch.stats["errors_server"] == 1


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_enrichment_cache.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты EnrichmentCache и TokenBucket.

Без сети, без провайдеров — только инфраструктура.
"""
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from paradox.integrations.base import EnrichmentResult
from paradox.integrations.cache import EnrichmentCache
from paradox.integrations.rate_limit import TokenBucket


def test_cache_put_get(tmp_path):
    """Базовый round-trip."""
    db = tmp_path / "enrichment.db"
    with EnrichmentCache(db) as cache:
        result = EnrichmentResult(
            source="shodan_internetdb",
            ioc="8.8.8.8",
            data={"ports": [53, 443], "hostnames": ["dns.google"]},
            fetched_at=time.time(),
            ttl_days=7,
        )
        cache.put(result)

        got = cache.get("shodan_internetdb", "8.8.8.8")
        assert got is not None
        assert got.source == "shodan_internetdb"
        assert got.ioc == "8.8.8.8"
        assert got.data["ports"] == [53, 443]


def test_cache_get_missing(tmp_path):
    """Нет записи — None."""
    db = tmp_path / "enrichment.db"
    with EnrichmentCache(db) as cache:
        assert cache.get("shodan_internetdb", "1.2.3.4") is None


def test_cache_get_stale(tmp_path):
    """Запись устарела — None."""
    db = tmp_path / "enrichment.db"
    with EnrichmentCache(db) as cache:
        old = EnrichmentResult(
            source="shodan_internetdb",
            ioc="8.8.8.8",
            data={"ports": [53]},
            fetched_at=time.time() - 10 * 86400,  # 10 дней назад
            ttl_days=7,
        )
        cache.put(old)

        assert cache.get("shodan_internetdb", "8.8.8.8") is None


def test_cache_put_overwrites(tmp_path):
    """UPSERT: повторный put меняет данные."""
    db = tmp_path / "enrichment.db"
    with EnrichmentCache(db) as cache:
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="shodan_internetdb", ioc="8.8.8.8",
            data={"ports": [53]}, fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
        ))
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="shodan_internetdb", ioc="8.8.8.8",
            data={"ports": [53, 443]}, fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
        ))

        got = cache.get("shodan_internetdb", "8.8.8.8")
        assert got.data["ports"] == [53, 443]


def test_cache_invalidate_old(tmp_path):
    """Очистка старых записей."""
    db = tmp_path / "enrichment.db"
    with EnrichmentCache(db) as cache:
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="x", ioc="old",
            data={}, fetched_at=time.time() - 40 * 86400, ttl_days=7,
        ))
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="x", ioc="new",
            data={}, fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
        ))

        deleted = cache.invalidate_old(older_than_days=30)
        assert deleted == 1
        assert cache.get("x", "new") is not None


def test_cache_stats(tmp_path):
    """Сводка: total, stale, by_source."""
    db = tmp_path / "enrichment.db"
    with EnrichmentCache(db) as cache:
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="shodan", ioc="1.1.1.1",
            data={}, fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
        ))
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="shodan", ioc="8.8.8.8",
            data={}, fetched_at=time.time() - 10 * 86400, ttl_days=7,
        ))
        cache.put(EnrichmentResult(
            source="vt", ioc="9.9.9.9",
            data={}, fetched_at=time.time(), ttl_days=7,
        ))

        stats = cache.stats()
        assert stats["total"] == 3
        assert stats["stale"] == 1
        assert stats["by_source"]["shodan"] == 2
        assert stats["by_source"]["vt"] == 1


def test_token_bucket_immediate():
    """Достаточно токенов — сразу True."""
    bucket = TokenBucket(capacity=5, refill_per_sec=1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
        assert bucket.try_acquire() is True
    assert bucket.try_acquire() is False


def test_token_bucket_refills():
    """Токены восполняются."""
    bucket = TokenBucket(capacity=1, refill_per_sec=100.0)
    assert bucket.try_acquire() is True
    assert bucket.try_acquire() is False
    time.sleep(0.05)  # 5 токенов должно восполниться (но capped на 1)
    assert bucket.try_acquire() is True


def test_token_bucket_invalid():
    """Валидация параметров."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        TokenBucket(capacity=0, refill_per_sec=1.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        TokenBucket(capacity=1, refill_per_sec=0)


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_basic.py
# ==========================================
from scapy.all import Ether, ARP, IP, UDP, DNS, DNSQR
from paradox.models import CaptureState
from paradox.parsers import PacketRecord
from paradox.extractors.dns import DNSExtractor
from paradox.extractors.arp_history import ARPMacHistoryExtractor


def test_dns_extractor_a_record():
    # Генерим реальный DNS-запрос через Scapy
    pkt = Ether() / IP(src="10.0.0.5", dst="8.8.8.8") / UDP(sport=12345, dport=53) / DNS(qd=DNSQR(qname="example.com"))

    # Создаем PacketRecord вручную, имитируя парсер
    record = PacketRecord(
        ts=100.0,
        length=len(pkt),
        raw=bytes(pkt),
        src_ip="10.0.0.5",
        dst_ip="8.8.8.8",
        proto="udp",
        src_port=12345,
        dst_port=53,
        payload=bytes(pkt[UDP].payload)  # DNS-часть
    )

    state = CaptureState()
    DNSExtractor().process(record, state)

    assert "example.com" in state.dns_queries
    assert state.dns_queries["example.com"] == 1
    assert "10.0.0.5" in state.dns_queries_by_src


def test_arp_history_extractor():
    pkt = Ether() / ARP(op=1, psrc="192.168.1.10", hwsrc="aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff", pdst="192.168.1.1")

    record = PacketRecord(
        ts=200.0,
        length=len(pkt),
        raw=bytes(pkt),
        proto="arp",
        arp_op=1,
        arp_sender_ip="192.168.1.10",
        arp_sender_mac="aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff"
    )

    state = CaptureState()
    ARPMacHistoryExtractor().process(record, state)

    assert "192.168.1.10" in state.arp_macs
    assert "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff" in state.arp_macs["192.168.1.10"]
    assert state.arp_macs["192.168.1.10"]["aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff"][2] == 1  # count


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_dns.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты DNS-экстрактора."""
from scapy.all import Ether, IP, UDP, DNS, DNSQR, DNSRR
from paradox.models import CaptureState
from paradox.parsers import PacketRecord
from paradox.extractors.dns import DNSExtractor


def _make_dns_record(qname: str, rcode: int = 0, answers: list | None = None) -> PacketRecord:
    """Собирает PacketRecord из реального DNS-пакета."""
    if answers:
        dns = DNS(qd=DNSQR(qname=qname), an=answers, rcode=rcode)
    else:
        dns = DNS(qd=DNSQR(qname=qname), rcode=rcode)

    pkt = Ether() / IP(src="10.0.0.5", dst="8.8.8.8") / UDP(sport=12345, dport=53) / dns

    return PacketRecord(
        ts=100.0,
        length=len(pkt),
        raw=bytes(pkt),
        src_ip="10.0.0.5",
        dst_ip="8.8.8.8",
        proto="udp",
        src_port=12345,
        dst_port=53,
        payload=bytes(pkt[UDP].payload),
    )


def test_dns_query_recorded():
    """Обычный DNS-запрос сохраняется в state."""
    record = _make_dns_record("example.com")
    state = CaptureState()
    DNSExtractor().process(record, state)

    assert state.dns_queries["example.com"] == 1
    assert state.dns_queries_by_src["10.0.0.5"]["example.com"] == 1


def test_dns_nxdomain_recorded():
    """NXDOMAIN увеличивает dns_nxdomain и dns_nxdomain_ts_by_src."""
    record = _make_dns_record("nonexistent.invalid", rcode=3)
    state = CaptureState()
    DNSExtractor().process(record, state)

    assert state.dns_nxdomain["nonexistent.invalid"] == 1
    assert state.dns_nxdomain_by_src["10.0.0.5"] == 1
    assert len(state.dns_nxdomain_ts_by_src["10.0.0.5"]) == 1


def test_dns_non_ascii_ignored():
    """Битый qname (не ASCII) — не падаем и не сохраняем."""
    # Явные MAC обязательны: без них Scapy резолвит interface
    # (на Windows — "Microsoft KM-TEST Loopback Adapter", которого
    # может не быть), и Ether().build() падает с ValueError.
    dns = DNS(qd=DNSQR(qname=b"\xff\xfe.bad"))
    pkt = (
        Ether(src="00:11:22:33:44:55", dst="00:11:22:33:44:66")
        / IP(src="10.0.0.5", dst="8.8.8.8")
        / UDP(sport=12345, dport=53)
        / dns
    )
    record = PacketRecord(
        ts=100.0, length=len(pkt), raw=bytes(pkt),
        src_ip="10.0.0.5", dst_ip="8.8.8.8",
        proto="udp", src_port=12345, dst_port=53,
        payload=bytes(pkt[UDP].payload),
    )

    state = CaptureState()
    DNSExtractor().process(record, state)
    assert len(state.dns_queries) == 0


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_http.py
# ==========================================
"""
Unit-тесты HTTPExtractor.
"""
import pytest

from paradox.extractors.flow import FlowExtractor
from paradox.extractors.http import HTTPExtractor
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey
from paradox.parsers import PacketRecord


def _make_tcp_record(src_ip, dst_ip, sport, dport, payload):
    return PacketRecord(
        ts=1.0,
        length=54 + len(payload),
        raw=b"",
        src_ip=src_ip,
        dst_ip=dst_ip,
        src_port=sport,
        dst_port=dport,
        proto="tcp",
        payload=payload,
    )


def _make_request(method="GET", uri="/", host="example.com",
                  user_agent="Mozilla/5.0"):
    lines = [
        f"{method} {uri} HTTP/1.1",
        f"Host: {host}",
        f"User-Agent: {user_agent}",
        "",
        "",
    ]
    return "\r\n".join(lines).encode("latin-1")


# ---------- основной сценарий ----------

def test_http_extractor_writes_flow_fields():
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 80,
        _make_request(uri="/index.html", host="example.com"),
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="192.168.1.100",
                  dst_ip="203.0.113.5", src_port=54000, dst_port=80)
    flow = state.flows[key]

    assert flow.http_method == "GET"
    assert flow.http_uri == "/index.html"
    assert flow.http_host == "example.com"
    assert flow.http_user_agent == "Mozilla/5.0"


def test_http_extractor_on_non_standard_port():
    """HTTP на 8080 — тоже ловим."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 8080,
        _make_request(uri="/admin"),
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="192.168.1.100",
                  dst_ip="203.0.113.5", src_port=54000, dst_port=8080)
    assert state.flows[key].http_uri == "/admin"


# ---------- игнор ----------

def test_http_extractor_ignores_response():
    """Ответ сервера (HTTP/1.1 200 OK) — не запрос, не пишем."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "203.0.113.5", "192.168.1.100", 80, 54000,
        b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 5\r\n\r\nhello",
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="203.0.113.5",
                  dst_ip="192.168.1.100", src_port=80, dst_port=54000)
    assert state.flows[key].http_method is None


def test_http_extractor_ignores_tls():
    """TLS handshake — не HTTP."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 443,
        b"\x16\x03\x01\x00\x50\x01\x00\x00\x4c\x03\x03",
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="192.168.1.100",
                  dst_ip="203.0.113.5", src_port=54000, dst_port=443)
    assert state.flows[key].http_method is None


def test_http_extractor_ignores_random_binary():
    """Бинарный мусор — не HTTP."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 80,
        b"\x00\x01\x02\x03\x04\x05",
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="192.168.1.100",
                  dst_ip="203.0.113.5", src_port=54000, dst_port=80)
    assert state.flows[key].http_method is None


# ---------- перезапись ----------

def test_http_extractor_does_not_overwrite():
    """Второй HTTP-запрос в flow не перезаписывает первый."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec1 = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 80,
        _make_request(uri="/first"),
    )
    rec2 = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 80,
        _make_request(uri="/second"),
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec1, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec1, state)
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec2, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="192.168.1.100",
                  dst_ip="203.0.113.5", src_port=54000, dst_port=80)
    assert state.flows[key].http_uri == "/first"


# ---------- без flow ----------

def test_http_extractor_raises_without_flow():
    """Без FlowExtractor — явная ошибка."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 80,
        _make_request(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="FlowExtractor"):
        HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)


def test_http_extractor_skips_udp():
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = PacketRecord(
        ts=1.0, length=100, raw=b"",
        src_ip="a", dst_ip="b",
        src_port=100, dst_port=80,
        proto="udp",
        payload=_make_request(),
    )
    HTTPExtractor().process(rec, state)

    assert len(state.flows) == 0


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_http_object.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты HTTPObjectExtractor — сборка HTTP из TCP-потоков."""
from dataclasses import dataclass

from paradox.extractors.http_object import HTTPObjectExtractor
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey


@dataclass
class _FakeConfig:
    """Минимальный Config для экстрактора.

    НЕ используем MagicMock: getattr(MagicMock(), 'attr', default)
    возвращает MagicMock (не default), и http_object.py падает
    на `int > MagicMock`.
    """
    http_object_body_limit: int = 1024 * 1024


def _key(src, dst, sport, dport):
    return FlowKey("tcp", src, dst, sport, dport)


def test_http_object_request_and_response_paired():
    """Парный request+response: у response есть URI из request."""
    state = CaptureState()
    client_key = _key("10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 80)
    server_key = _key("1.1.1.1", "10.0.0.5", 80, 54321)

    state.tcp_streams[client_key] = (
        b"GET /index.html HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: example.com\r\n"
        b"User-Agent: curl/8.0\r\n\r\n"
    )
    state.tcp_streams[server_key] = (
        b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: text/html\r\n"
        b"Content-Length: 5\r\n\r\nHELLO"
    )

    HTTPObjectExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=_FakeConfig())

    assert len(state.http_objects) == 2
    kinds = [obj.kind for _, obj in state.http_objects]
    assert "request" in kinds
    assert "response" in kinds

    # response получил URI из парного request
    for k, obj in state.http_objects:
        if obj.kind == "response":
            assert obj.uri == "/index.html"
            assert obj.body == b"HELLO"
            assert obj.status_code == 200


def test_http_object_only_request():
    """Только request (без response) — один объект."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = _key("10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 80)
    state.tcp_streams[key] = b"POST /api HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x.com\r\n\r\n"
    HTTPObjectExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=_FakeConfig())

    assert len(state.http_objects) == 1
    _, obj = state.http_objects[0]
    assert obj.kind == "request"
    assert obj.method == "POST"
    assert obj.uri == "/api"


def test_http_object_non_http_stream_ignored():
    """Не-HTTP поток (TLS handshake) — пропускается."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = _key("10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 443)
    state.tcp_streams[key] = b"\x16\x03\x01\x00\x50 FAKE_TLS"
    HTTPObjectExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=_FakeConfig())
    assert state.http_objects == []


def test_http_object_empty_streams_noop():
    """Пустой state — ничего не происходит."""
    state = CaptureState()
    HTTPObjectExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=_FakeConfig())
    assert state.http_objects == []


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_ndp.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты NDP-экстрактора."""
import ipaddress
import struct
from dataclasses import dataclass

from paradox.extractors.ndp import NDPExtractor


@dataclass
class MockRecord:
    ts: float
    src_ip: str
    dst_ip: str
    ip_proto: int
    payload: bytes


class MockState:
    pass


def _mac_option(opt_type, mac_str):
    mac_bytes = bytes.fromhex(mac_str.replace(":", ""))
    return struct.pack("!BB", opt_type, 1) + mac_bytes


def test_ndp_extractor():
    state = MockState()
    extractor = NDPExtractor()

    # NA (neighbor advertisement)
    flags = 0x60
    target_ip = ipaddress.IPv6Address("2001:db8::2").packed
    p1 = struct.pack("!BBH", 136, 0, 0) + struct.pack("!B3x", flags) + target_ip
    p1 += _mac_option(2, "00:11:22:33:44:55")
    r1 = MockRecord(1000.0, "2001:db8::2", "2001:db8::1", 58, p1)

    # RA (router advertisement)
    p2 = struct.pack("!BBHBBHII", 134, 0, 0, 64, 0, 1800, 0, 0)
    p2 += _mac_option(1, "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff")
    r2 = MockRecord(1001.0, "fe80::1", "ff02::1", 58, p2)

    extractor.process(r1, state)
    extractor.process(r2, state)

    assert state.ndp_total == 2
    assert state.ndp_neighbors["2001:db8::2"] == "00:11:22:33:44:55"
    assert "fe80::1" in state.ndp_routers


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_tls.py
# ==========================================
"""
Unit-тесты TLSExtractor.

Работаем с PacketRecord и CaptureState напрямую. Никакого pcap.
"""
import pytest

from paradox.extractors.flow import FlowExtractor
from paradox.extractors.tls import TLSExtractor
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey
from paradox.parsers import PacketRecord


# минимальный валидный ClientHello с example.com
def _build_client_hello(sni: str) -> bytes:
    sni_bytes = sni.encode("ascii")
    name_entry = (
        b"\x00" + len(sni_bytes).to_bytes(2, "big") + sni_bytes
    )
    server_name_list = len(name_entry).to_bytes(2, "big") + name_entry
    server_name_ext = (
        b"\x00\x00"
        + len(server_name_list).to_bytes(2, "big")
        + server_name_list
    )
    extensions_block = (
        len(server_name_ext).to_bytes(2, "big") + server_name_ext
    )
    body = (
        b"\x03\x03"
        + b"\x00" * 32
        + b"\x00"
        + b"\x00\x02\x13\x01"
        + b"\x01\x00"
        + extensions_block
    )
    handshake = b"\x01" + len(body).to_bytes(3, "big") + body
    return b"\x16\x03\x01" + len(handshake).to_bytes(2, "big") + handshake


def _make_tcp_record(
    src_ip: str, dst_ip: str, sport: int, dport: int, payload: bytes,
) -> PacketRecord:
    return PacketRecord(
        ts=1.0,
        length=54 + len(payload),
        raw=b"",
        src_ip=src_ip,
        dst_ip=dst_ip,
        src_port=sport,
        dst_port=dport,
        proto="tcp",
        payload=payload,
    )


# ---------- основной сценарий ----------

def test_tls_extractor_writes_sni():
    state = CaptureState()
    flow_ext = FlowExtractor()
    tls_ext = TLSExtractor()

    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "192.168.1.100", "203.0.113.5", 54000, 443,
        _build_client_hello("example.com"),
    )

    flow_ext.process(rec, state)
    tls_ext.process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(
        proto="tcp",
        src_ip="192.168.1.100", dst_ip="203.0.113.5",
        src_port=54000, dst_port=443,
    )
    assert state.flows[key].sni == "example.com"


def test_tls_extractor_on_non_standard_port():
    """TLS на нестандартном порту — тоже ловим."""
    state = CaptureState()
    FlowExtractor().process(
        _make_tcp_record("a", "b", 100, 8443, b""), state,
    )
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "a", "b", 100, 8443, _build_client_hello("c2.evil.com"),
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec, state)
    TLSExtractor().process(rec, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="a", dst_ip="b",
                  src_port=100, dst_port=8443)
    assert state.flows[key].sni == "c2.evil.com"


# ---------- edge cases ----------

def test_tls_extractor_ignores_non_tls():
    state = CaptureState()
    FlowExtractor().process(
        _make_tcp_record("a", "b", 100, 80, b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n"),
        state,
    )
    TLSExtractor().process(
        _make_tcp_record("a", "b", 100, 80, b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n"),
        state,
    )

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="a", dst_ip="b",
                  src_port=100, dst_port=80)
    assert state.flows[key].sni is None


def test_tls_extractor_does_not_overwrite_sni():
    """Второе ClientHello не перезаписывает уже установленный SNI."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec1 = _make_tcp_record(
        "a", "b", 100, 443, _build_client_hello("first.com"),
    )
    rec2 = _make_tcp_record(
        "a", "b", 100, 443, _build_client_hello("second.com"),
    )
    FlowExtractor().process(rec1, state)
    TLSExtractor().process(rec1, state)
    TLSExtractor().process(rec2, state)

    key = FlowKey(proto="tcp", src_ip="a", dst_ip="b",
                  src_port=100, dst_port=443)
    assert state.flows[key].sni == "first.com"


def test_tls_extractor_raises_without_flow():
    """Если FlowExtractor не запускался — TLSExtractor падает явно."""
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = _make_tcp_record(
        "a", "b", 100, 443, _build_client_hello("example.com"),
    )

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="FlowExtractor"):
        TLSExtractor().process(rec, state)


def test_tls_extractor_skips_udp():
    state = CaptureState()
    rec = PacketRecord(
        ts=1.0, length=100, raw=b"",
        src_ip="a", dst_ip="b",
        src_port=100, dst_port=443,
        proto="udp",
        payload=_build_client_hello("example.com"),
    )

    TLSExtractor().process(rec, state)

    # никаких падений, никаких flows
    assert len(state.flows) == 0


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_extractors_tls_cert.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты TlsCertificateExtractor.

Проверяем обход flows и сборку state.tls_certs. Парсинг X.509
мокаем — он уже покрыт отдельными тестами в test_x509_cert.py
и test_tls_cert.py.
"""
from unittest.mock import patch

from paradox.extractors.tls_cert import TlsCertificateExtractor
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats


def _make_flow(sni: str | None = None) -> FlowStats:
    f = FlowStats()
    f.sni = sni
    return f


def test_tls_cert_extractor_happy_path():
    """Есть SNI + обратный поток → сертификат извлекается и сохраняется."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey("tcp", "10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 443)
    state.flows[key] = _make_flow(sni="example.com")

    reverse_key = FlowKey("tcp", "1.1.1.1", "10.0.0.5", 443, 54321)
    state.tcp_streams[reverse_key] = b"FAKE_TLS_STREAM"

    fake_cert = object()

    with patch(
        "paradox.extractors.tls_cert.parse_certificate_from_stream",
        return_value=fake_cert,
    ) as mock_parse:
        TlsCertificateExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=None)

    assert len(state.tls_certs) == 1
    assert state.tls_certs[0] == (key, fake_cert)
    mock_parse.assert_called_once_with(b"FAKE_TLS_STREAM")


def test_tls_cert_extractor_no_sni():
    """Flow без SNI → пропускается, парсер не вызывается."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey("tcp", "10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 443)
    state.flows[key] = _make_flow(sni=None)

    with patch(
        "paradox.extractors.tls_cert.parse_certificate_from_stream",
    ) as mock_parse:
        TlsCertificateExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=None)

    assert state.tls_certs == []
    mock_parse.assert_not_called()


def test_tls_cert_extractor_no_reverse_stream():
    """SNI есть, но обратного потока в tcp_streams нет → пропускаем."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey("tcp", "10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 443)
    state.flows[key] = _make_flow(sni="example.com")
    # Обратного потока нет

    with patch(
        "paradox.extractors.tls_cert.parse_certificate_from_stream",
    ) as mock_parse:
        TlsCertificateExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=None)

    assert state.tls_certs == []
    mock_parse.assert_not_called()


def test_tls_cert_extractor_parse_fails():
    """Парсер вернул None → не падаем, ничего не сохраняем."""
    state = CaptureState()
    key = FlowKey("tcp", "10.0.0.5", "1.1.1.1", 54321, 443)
    state.flows[key] = _make_flow(sni="example.com")

    reverse_key = FlowKey("tcp", "1.1.1.1", "10.0.0.5", 443, 54321)
    state.tcp_streams[reverse_key] = b"BAD_BYTES"

    with patch(
        "paradox.extractors.tls_cert.parse_certificate_from_stream",
        return_value=None,
    ):
        TlsCertificateExtractor().run_post_extract(state, config=None)

    assert state.tls_certs == []


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_facts.py
# ==========================================
from paradox.contradictions.facts import extract_attack_facts, extract_arp_facts
from paradox.models import Finding, CaptureState


def test_extract_attack_facts():
    findings = [
        Finding(type="brute_force", value="1.1.1.1 -> 2.2.2.2", confidence=0.9, src_ip="1.1.1.1", dst_ip="2.2.2.2"),
        Finding(type="large_transfer", value="A <-> B", confidence=0.8, src_ip="3.3.3.3", dst_ip="4.4.4.4",
                evidence={"direction": "outbound"})
    ]
    facts = extract_attack_facts(findings)

    # Проверяем, что из brute_force извлеклись роли
    assert any(f.predicate == "attacker_of" and f.subject == "1.1.1.1" for f in facts)
    assert any(f.predicate == "victim_of" and f.subject == "2.2.2.2" for f in facts)

    # Проверяем large_transfer (outbound = victim exfil)
    assert any(f.predicate == "victim_of" and f.subject == "3.3.3.3" for f in facts)


def test_extract_arp_facts():
    state = CaptureState()
    state.arp_macs = {
        "192.168.1.1": {
            "aa:bb:cc:dd:ee:ff": [100.0, 150.0, 5],  # 5 пакетов
            "11:22:33:44:55:66": [160.0, 200.0, 2],  # 2 пакета
        }
    }
    facts = extract_arp_facts(state)
    assert len(facts) == 2
    assert facts[0].subject == "192.168.1.1"
    assert facts[0].predicate == "has_mac"
    assert facts[0].confidence == 0.9  # 5 пакетов -> уверенность 0.9
    assert facts[1].confidence == 0.6  # 2 пакета -> уверенность 0.6


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_false_positives.py
# ==========================================
import pytest
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.detectors.large_transfer import LargeTransferDetector
from paradox.detectors.dga import DGADetector
from paradox.detectors.sni_mismatch import SNIMismatchDetector


@pytest.fixture
def config():
    return Config()


def test_large_transfer_windows_update_fp(config):
    """Windows Update не должен считаться утечкой."""
    det = LargeTransferDetector()
    state = CaptureState()

    key = FlowKey("tcp", "192.168.1.10", "13.107.4.50", 12345, 443)
    flow = FlowStats()
    flow.bytes_sent = 150 * 1024 * 1024  # 150 МБ
    flow.http_host = "download.microsoft.com"
    flow.http_user_agent = "Microsoft BITS/7.8"
    state.flows[key] = flow

    findings = det.run(state, config)
    assert len(findings) == 0, "Large transfer сработал на Windows Update (FP)"


def test_dga_allowlist_fp(config):
    """Легитимные CDN с длинными именами не должны триггерить DGA."""
    det = DGADetector()
    state = CaptureState()

    state.dns_queries["d3js8k5g2m9x8v.cloudfront.net"] = 1
    state.dns_queries["secure-connect-12345.amazonaws.com"] = 1

    findings = det.run(state, config)
    assert len(findings) == 0, "DGA сработал на легитимный CDN (FP)"

"""Тесты описаний для tooltip'ов."""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox_gui.field_help import (
    FIELD_HELP,
    SECTION_BY_TITLE,
    SECTION_HELP,
    get_field_help,
    get_section_help,
)


def test_field_help_non_empty():
    assert len(FIELD_HELP) >= 40, f"описаний слишком мало: {len(FIELD_HELP)}"


def test_field_help_describes_key_fields():
    required = {
        "beaconing_min_connections",
        "nxdomain_burst_window_sec",
        "dga_entropy_threshold",
        "large_transfer_min_bytes",
        "output_dir",
    }
    missing = required - set(FIELD_HELP)
    assert not missing, f"нет описаний для: {sorted(missing)}"


def test_field_help_keys_exist_in_config():
    cfg = Config()
    unknown = [k for k in FIELD_HELP if not hasattr(cfg, k)]
    assert not unknown, f"описаны поля, которых нет в Config: {unknown}"


def test_get_field_help_fallback():
    text = get_field_help("no_such_field_xyz")
    assert "no_such_field_xyz" in text


def test_section_help_non_empty():
    assert len(SECTION_HELP) >= 10


def test_section_by_title_covers_all_sections():
    for key in ("thresholds", "detectors", "contradictions",
                "allowlist", "dga", "brute", "network",
                "reports", "threatintel", "perf", "general"):
        assert key in SECTION_HELP, f"нет описания секции {key}"


def test_get_section_help_known():
    assert "beaconing" in get_section_help("thresholds").lower() or            "порог" in get_section_help("thresholds").lower()

"""Тесты подсказок справа от полей."""
import os

    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    yield app

def view(qapp, tmp_path):
    from paradox_gui.settings_store import SettingsStore
    from paradox_gui.views.settings_view import SettingsView
    return SettingsView(store=SettingsStore(tmp_path / "s.json"))

    assert key in view._field_hints
