# FILE: tests/test_story.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты анализа истории хоста."""
from paradox.analyzers.story import build_host_story


def test_story_empty():
    report = {}
    res = build_host_story(report, "10.0.0.1")
    assert res["target_ip"] == "10.0.0.1"
    assert res["events"] == []
    assert res["summary"]["total_events"] == 0


def test_story_events_sorted():
    report = {
        "flows": {
            "top_by_bytes": [
                {"src_ip": "10.0.0.1", "dst_ip": "8.8.8.8", "first_ts": 100},
                {"src_ip": "10.0.0.2", "dst_ip": "10.0.0.1", "first_ts": 50},
            ]
        },
        "findings": [
            {"type": "dga", "src_ip": "10.0.0.1", "first_seen": 75}
        ]
    }

    res = build_host_story(report, "10.0.0.1")

    assert res["summary"]["total_events"] == 3
    assert res["summary"]["flows_count"] == 2
    assert res["summary"]["findings_count"] == 1
    assert res["summary"]["unique_contacts"] == 2

    # Проверка сортировки: 50 -> 75 -> 100
    assert res["events"][0]["ts"] == 50
    assert res["events"][0]["type"] == "flow"

    assert res["events"][1]["ts"] == 75
    assert res["events"][1]["type"] == "finding"

    assert res["events"][2]["ts"] == 100
    assert res["events"][2]["type"] == "flow"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_timeline.py
# ==========================================
"""
Тесты timeline / kill-chain reconstruction.

Проверяем:
  - детерминированность и сортировку build_timeline
  - маппинг тип finding → фаза
  - маппинг rule contradiction → фаза
  - edge cases (пустой вход, отсутствие ts, пустые facts)
  - summarize_by_phase

v0.8.0: timeline tests
"""
from paradox.contradictions.base import Contradiction, Fact
from paradox.models import Finding
from paradox.timeline import (
    TimelineEvent,
    build_timeline,
    summarize_by_phase,
)


# ---------- helpers ----------

def _f(ftype, ts, src="1.1.1.1", dst="2.2.2.2", **kw):
    return Finding(
        type=ftype, value="v", confidence=kw.pop("confidence", 0.8),
        src_ip=src, dst_ip=dst,
        first_seen=ts,
        severity=kw.pop("severity", "medium"),
        mitre_techniques=kw.pop("mitre_techniques", ()),
        **kw,
    )


def _c(rule, subject, facts_ts, severity="critical", confidence=0.8):
    facts = [
        Fact(
            source="finding", subject=subject,
            predicate="victim_of", value="x",
            ts=ts, confidence=0.8, evidence={},
        )
        for ts in facts_ts
    ]
    return Contradiction(
        rule=rule, subject=subject, facts=facts,
        severity=severity, confidence=confidence,
        likely_causes=[], resolution_hint=None,
    )


# ---------- пустой вход ----------

def test_empty_findings_empty_timeline():
    assert build_timeline([]) == []


def test_empty_with_none_contradictions():
    assert build_timeline([], None) == []


def test_empty_with_empty_contradictions():
    assert build_timeline([], []) == []


# ---------- finding без first_seen ----------

def test_finding_without_ts_skipped():
    f = Finding(type="brute_force", value="v", confidence=0.8)
    assert build_timeline([f]) == []


def test_mixed_some_without_ts():
    f1 = _f("brute_force", 100.0)
    f2 = Finding(type="port_scan", value="v", confidence=0.7)  # без ts
    f3 = _f("beaconing", 200.0)
    events = build_timeline([f1, f2, f3])
    assert len(events) == 2
    assert {e.type for e in events} == {"brute_force", "beaconing"}


# ---------- фазы ----------

def test_phase_recon():
    events = build_timeline([_f("port_scan", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "recon"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 10


def test_phase_initial_access():
    events = build_timeline([_f("brute_force", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "initial_access"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 20


def test_phase_execution():
    events = build_timeline([_f("command_injection", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "execution"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 30


def test_phase_c2():
    events = build_timeline([_f("beaconing", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "c2"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 60


def test_phase_lateral_movement():
    events = build_timeline([_f("lateral_movement", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "lateral_movement"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 50


def test_phase_exfiltration():
    events = build_timeline([_f("large_transfer", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "exfiltration"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 70


def test_unknown_type_goes_to_other():
    events = build_timeline([_f("totally_new_type", 1.0)])
    assert events[0].phase == "other"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 999


# ---------- сортировка ----------

def test_sorted_by_ts():
    events = build_timeline([
        _f("beaconing", 300.0),
        _f("port_scan", 100.0),
        _f("brute_force", 200.0),
    ])
    assert [e.ts for e in events] == [100.0, 200.0, 300.0]


def test_same_ts_sorted_by_chain_order():
    # Один ts, разные фазы. recon (10) → c2 (60).
    events = build_timeline([
        _f("beaconing", 100.0, src="b"),
        _f("port_scan", 100.0, src="a"),
    ])
    assert events[0].type == "port_scan"
    assert events[1].type == "beaconing"


def test_same_ts_same_order_sorted_by_subject():
    # port_scan и web_scanner — обе recon (10), разные subject.
    events = build_timeline([
        _f("web_scanner", 100.0, src="z"),
        _f("port_scan", 100.0, src="a"),
    ])
    assert events[0].subject == "a"
    assert events[1].subject == "z"


def test_deterministic_order():
    """Одинаковый вход — одинаковый выход (несколько раз)."""
    findings = [
        _f("beaconing", 300.0, src="z"),
        _f("port_scan", 100.0, src="a"),
        _f("brute_force", 100.0, src="a"),
        _f("lateral_movement", 200.0, src="m"),
    ]
    runs = [build_timeline(findings) for _ in range(3)]
    sigs = [[(e.ts, e.type, e.subject) for e in r] for r in runs]
    assert sigs[0] == sigs[1] == sigs[2]


# ---------- contradictions ----------

def test_contradiction_event_ts_from_max_facts():
    c = _c("victim_makes_attack", "9.9.9.9", [10.0, 42.0, 20.0])
    events = build_timeline([], [c])
    assert len(events) == 1
    assert events[0].ts == 42.0
    assert events[0].kind == "contradiction"
    assert events[0].type == "victim_makes_attack"
    assert events[0].phase == "lateral_movement"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 50


def test_contradiction_without_facts_skipped():
    c = _c("mac_ip_conflict", "1.1.1.1", [])
    assert build_timeline([], [c]) == []


def test_contradiction_zero_ts_skipped():
    # Все facts с ts=0 → событие пропускается.
    c = _c("mac_ip_conflict", "1.1.1.1", [0.0, 0.0])
    assert build_timeline([], [c]) == []


def test_contradiction_phase_mac_ip_conflict():
    c = _c("mac_ip_conflict", "1.1.1.1", [10.0])
    events = build_timeline([], [c])
    assert events[0].phase == "initial_access"


def test_contradiction_phase_reverse_asymmetry():
    c = _c("reverse_asymmetry", "1.1.1.1", [10.0])
    events = build_timeline([], [c])
    assert events[0].phase == "exfiltration"


def test_unknown_rule_goes_to_other():
    c = _c("brand_new_rule", "1.1.1.1", [10.0])
    events = build_timeline([], [c])
    assert events[0].phase == "other"
    assert events[0].chain_order == 999


# ---------- смешанный вход ----------

def test_mixed_findings_and_contradictions():
    f = _f("brute_force", 100.0)
    c = _c("victim_makes_attack", "1.1.1.1", [150.0])
    events = build_timeline([f], [c])
    assert len(events) == 2
    assert events[0].kind == "finding"
    assert events[1].kind == "contradiction"
    assert [e.ts for e in events] == [100.0, 150.0]


# ---------- summarize_by_phase ----------

def test_summarize_empty():
    assert summarize_by_phase([]) == []


def test_summarize_groups_and_orders():
    events = build_timeline([
        _f("port_scan", 1.0),          # recon
        _f("brute_force", 2.0),        # initial_access
        _f("command_injection", 3.0),  # execution
        _f("large_transfer", 4.0),     # exfiltration
    ])
    grouped = summarize_by_phase(events)
    phases = [g["phase"] for g in grouped]
    assert phases == ["recon", "initial_access", "execution", "exfiltration"]
    for g in grouped:
        assert g["count"] == 1
        assert isinstance(g["events"], list)
        assert len(g["events"]) == g["count"]


def test_summarize_groups_multiple_events():
    events = build_timeline([
        _f("port_scan", 1.0, src="a"),
        _f("web_scanner", 2.0, src="b"),
        _f("beaconing", 3.0),
    ])
    grouped = summarize_by_phase(events)
    by_phase = {g["phase"]: g for g in grouped}
    assert by_phase["recon"]["count"] == 2
    assert by_phase["c2"]["count"] == 1


def test_summarize_unknown_at_end():
    events = build_timeline([
        _f("unknown_type", 1.0),
        _f("port_scan", 2.0),
    ])
    grouped = summarize_by_phase(events)
    # other (999) идёт после recon (10).
    assert grouped[0]["phase"] == "recon"
    assert grouped[-1]["phase"] == "other"

def test_event_to_dict_keys():
    events = build_timeline([_f("port_scan", 1.0)])
    d = events[0].to_dict()
    for key in (
        "ts", "kind", "type", "subject", "severity",
        "confidence", "phase", "chain_order",
        "description", "mitre_id", "ref_id",
    ):
        assert key in d, f"missing {key}"

    f = _f("command_injection", 1.0, mitre_techniques=("T1190", "T1059"))
    events = build_timeline([f])
    assert events[0].mitre_id == "T1190"


def test_event_no_mitre_when_empty():

    events = build_timeline([f])
    assert events[0].mitre_id is None

    f = _f("port_scan", 1.0, src="10.0.0.5")
    events = build_timeline([f])
    assert events[0].subject == "10.0.0.5"

# ---------- v0.9.0: carved_file в timeline ----------

class _Carved:
    """Заглушка CarvedFile для тестов."""
    def __init__(self, ts, uri="a.sh", size=100, ext=".sh",
                 sha256="deadbeef" * 8, dst_ip="192.168.1.1"):
        self.first_ts = ts
        self.uri = uri
        self.size = size
        self.ext = ext
        self.sha256 = sha256
        self.dst_ip = dst_ip


def test_carved_file_event_created():
    cf = _Carved(ts=100.0, uri="/payload.sh")
    events = build_timeline([], [], carved_files=[cf])
    assert len(events) == 1
    e = events[0]
    assert e.kind == "carved_file"
    assert e.type == "carved_file"
    assert e.phase == "execution"
    assert e.chain_order == 30
    assert e.mitre_id == "T1105"
    assert "/payload.sh" in e.description


def test_carved_file_no_ts_skipped():
    cf = _Carved(ts=None)
    events = build_timeline([], [], carved_files=[cf])
    assert events == []


def test_carved_file_zero_ts_skipped():
    cf = _Carved(ts=0.0)
    events = build_timeline([], [], carved_files=[cf])
    assert events == []


def test_carved_file_mixed_with_findings():
    f = _f("brute_force", 50.0, src="10.0.0.1")
    cf = _Carved(ts=100.0, uri="/x.ps1")
    events = build_timeline([f], [], carved_files=[cf])
    assert len(events) == 2
    assert events[0].ts == 50.0
    assert events[1].ts == 100.0
    assert events[1].kind == "carved_file"


def test_carved_file_backward_compat_no_arg():
    """build_timeline(findings, contradictions) без carved_files — ок."""
    f = _f("port_scan", 10.0)
    events = build_timeline([f], [])
    assert len(events) == 1


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_tls_cert.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты парсера TLS Certificate handshake."""
from paradox.tls_cert import parse_certificate_from_stream
from tests.test_x509_cert import (
    _cert,
    _ctx,
    _int,
    _null,
    _oid,
    _octet,
    _seq,
    _tlv,
)


def _tls_record(handshake_bytes: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Обернуть handshake-байты в TLS record (content_type=0x16)."""
    length = len(handshake_bytes)
    return (
        bytes([0x16, 0x03, 0x03, (length >> 8) & 0xFF, length & 0xFF])
        + handshake_bytes
    )


def _handshake_message(hs_type: int, body: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Обернуть body в TLS handshake message."""
    n = len(body)
    return (
        bytes([hs_type, (n >> 16) & 0xFF, (n >> 8) & 0xFF, n & 0xFF])
        + body
    )


def _certificate_body_tls12(cert_der: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Certificate body в формате TLS 1.2."""
    cert_len = len(cert_der)
    chain = (cert_len.to_bytes(3, "big") + cert_der)
    return len(chain).to_bytes(3, "big") + chain


def _certificate_body_tls13(cert_der: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Certificate body в формате TLS 1.3 (пустой request_context)."""
    cert_len = len(cert_der)
    chain = (cert_len.to_bytes(3, "big") + cert_der)
    return (
        b"\x00"
        + len(chain).to_bytes(3, "big")
        + chain
        + b"\x00\x00"
    )


def _stream_with_certificate(cert_der: bytes, tls13: bool = False) -> bytes:
    """Полный TCP-поток: ServerHello + Certificate."""
    server_hello = _handshake_message(2, b"")
    if tls13:
        body = _certificate_body_tls13(cert_der)
    else:
        body = _certificate_body_tls12(cert_der)
    cert_msg = _handshake_message(11, body)
    return _tls_record(server_hello) + _tls_record(cert_msg)


def test_parse_tls12_certificate():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    stream = _stream_with_certificate(der, tls13=False)
    cert = parse_certificate_from_stream(stream)
    assert cert is not None
    assert cert.subject_cn == "example.com"
    assert cert.issuer_cn == "Test CA"


def test_parse_tls13_certificate():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    stream = _stream_with_certificate(der, tls13=True)
    cert = parse_certificate_from_stream(stream)
    assert cert is not None
    assert cert.subject_cn == "example.com"


def test_parse_cert_with_san():
    der = _cert(
        "example.com", "Test CA",
        san_dns=("example.com", "www.example.com"),
    )
    stream = _stream_with_certificate(der)
    cert = parse_certificate_from_stream(stream)
    assert cert is not None
    assert cert.san_dns == ["example.com", "www.example.com"]


def test_empty_stream():
    assert parse_certificate_from_stream(b"") is None


def test_non_tls_stream():
    assert parse_certificate_from_stream(b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n") is None


def test_truncated_record():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    stream = _stream_with_certificate(der)
    assert parse_certificate_from_stream(stream[:20]) is None


def test_no_certificate_message():
    server_hello = _handshake_message(2, b"")
    stream = _tls_record(server_hello)
    assert parse_certificate_from_stream(stream) is None


def test_two_handshake_in_one_record():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    sh = _handshake_message(2, b"")
    cm = _handshake_message(11, _certificate_body_tls12(der))
    stream = _tls_record(sh + cm)
    cert = parse_certificate_from_stream(stream)
    assert cert is not None
    assert cert.subject_cn == "example.com"


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_tls_parse.py
# ==========================================
"""
Unit-тесты парсера TLS ClientHello.

Эталонные ClientHello собраны вручную из реальных hex-дампов.
Проверяем извлечение SNI и edge cases.
"""
from paradox.tls_parse import extract_sni


def _build_client_hello(sni: str | None) -> bytes:
    """Собрать минимальный валидный ClientHello с заданным SNI."""
    # --- extensions ---
    if sni is not None:
        sni_bytes = sni.encode("ascii")
        server_name_data = (
            len(sni_bytes + b"").to_bytes(2, "big")  # placeholder, ниже поправим
        )
        # server_name list: name_type(1) + name_len(2) + name
        name_entry = (
            b"\x00"                                   # name_type = host_name
            + len(sni_bytes).to_bytes(2, "big")
            + sni_bytes
        )
        server_name_list = len(name_entry).to_bytes(2, "big") + name_entry
        server_name_ext = (
            b"\x00\x00"                               # ext type = server_name
            + len(server_name_list).to_bytes(2, "big")
            + server_name_list
        )
    else:
        server_name_ext = b""

    extensions = server_name_ext
    extensions_block = len(extensions).to_bytes(2, "big") + extensions

    # --- ClientHello body ---
    body = (
        b"\x03\x03"                 # client_version = TLS 1.2
        + b"\x00" * 32              # random
        + b"\x00"                   # session_id_len = 0
        + b"\x00\x02\x13\x01"       # cipher_suites: len=2, TLS_AES_128_GCM_SHA256
        + b"\x01\x00"               # compression: len=1, null
        + extensions_block
    )

    # --- handshake ---
    handshake = (
        b"\x01"                                   # msg_type = ClientHello
        + len(body).to_bytes(3, "big")
        + body
    )

    # --- TLS record ---
    record = (
        b"\x16"                                   # content_type = handshake
        + b"\x03\x01"                             # record version
        + len(handshake).to_bytes(2, "big")
        + handshake
    )

    return record


# ---------- основной сценарий ----------

def test_extract_simple_sni():
    payload = _build_client_hello("example.com")
    assert extract_sni(payload) == "example.com"


def test_extract_sni_lowercases():
    payload = _build_client_hello("EXAMPLE.COM")
    assert extract_sni(payload) == "example.com"


def test_extract_long_sni():
    sni = "very-long-subdomain.example-cdn-provider.com"
    payload = _build_client_hello(sni)
    assert extract_sni(payload) == sni


# ---------- edge cases ----------

def test_empty_payload():
    assert extract_sni(b"") is None


def test_too_short():
    assert extract_sni(b"\x16") is None


def test_not_handshake():
    """Не handshake-запись (например, application data 0x17)."""
    payload = b"\x17\x03\x03\x00\x05" + b"\x00" * 5
    assert extract_sni(payload) is None


def test_not_client_hello():
    """Handshake, но не ClientHello (например, ServerHello 0x02)."""
    body = b"\x00" * 10
    handshake = b"\x02" + len(body).to_bytes(3, "big") + body
    record = b"\x16\x03\x03" + len(handshake).to_bytes(2, "big") + handshake
    assert extract_sni(record) is None


def test_client_hello_without_sni():
    """Валидный ClientHello, но без server_name extension."""
    payload = _build_client_hello(None)
    assert extract_sni(payload) is None


def test_truncated_record():
    """Запись заявляет длину больше, чем есть в payload."""
    payload = b"\x16\x03\x03\xff\xff" + b"\x00" * 10
    assert extract_sni(payload) is None


def test_truncated_handshake():
    """Handshake заявляет длину больше, чем есть."""
    payload = b"\x16\x03\x03\x00\x0a" + b"\x01\xff\xff\xff" + b"\x00" * 2
    assert extract_sni(payload) is None


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_victim_makes_attack.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты правила victim_makes_attack (PIVOT)."""
from paradox.models import Finding
from paradox.contradictions.base import Fact
from paradox.contradictions.facts import extract_attack_facts
from paradox.contradictions.rules.e_victim_makes_attack import (
    VictimMakesAttackRule,
)


def _finding(ftype, src, dst, conf=0.85, ts=100.0):
    return Finding(
        type=ftype,
        value=f"{src} -> {dst}",
        confidence=conf,
        evidence={"src_ip": src, "dst_ip": dst},
        first_seen=ts,
    )


# 1. Полярность attack-типов: src=attacker, dst=victim
def test_extract_attack_facts_polarity():
    f = _finding("brute_force", "10.0.0.1", "192.168.1.5")
    facts = extract_attack_facts([f])
    preds = {(x.subject, x.predicate, x.value) for x in facts}
    assert ("192.168.1.5", "victim_of",   "10.0.0.1")  in preds
    assert ("10.0.0.1",   "attacker_of", "192.168.1.5") in preds


# 2. Полярность victim-signal-типов: src=victim, dst=C2
def test_extract_attack_facts_victim_signal():
    f = _finding("beaconing", "192.168.1.5", "1.2.3.4")
    facts = extract_attack_facts([f])
    preds = {(x.subject, x.predicate, x.value) for x in facts}
    assert ("192.168.1.5", "victim_of",   "1.2.3.4") in preds
    assert ("1.2.3.4",     "attacker_of", "192.168.1.5") in preds


# 3. Pivot: X — victim_of(A) и attacker_of(B) → critical, conf cap 0.90
def test_pivot_happy_path():
    facts = [
        Fact("finding", "192.168.1.12", "victim_of",   "10.0.1.50", 10.0, 0.95),
        Fact("finding", "192.168.1.12", "attacker_of", "192.168.1.1", 20.0, 0.90),
    ]
    rule = VictimMakesAttackRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    c = res[0]
    assert c.rule == "victim_makes_attack"
    assert c.subject == "192.168.1.12"
    assert c.severity == "critical"
    assert c.confidence == 0.90           # потолок
    assert len(c.facts) == 2
    assert c.priority == 0.90              # severity=1.0 × conf=0.90


# 4. Self-loop: X атакует X — не pivot
def test_self_loop_not_flagged():
    facts = [
        Fact("finding", "10.0.0.1", "victim_of",   "10.0.0.1", 10.0, 0.9),
        Fact("finding", "10.0.0.1", "attacker_of", "10.0.0.1", 10.0, 0.9),
    ]
    rule = VictimMakesAttackRule()
    assert rule.find_contradictions(facts) == []


# 5. Обратный порядок timestamps → hint про NAT/proxy
def test_reverse_timestamps_hint():
    facts = [
        Fact("finding", "10.0.0.5", "victim_of",   "10.0.0.9", 100.0, 0.9),
        Fact("finding", "10.0.0.5", "attacker_of", "10.0.0.3", 50.0,  0.9),
    ]
    rule = VictimMakesAttackRule()
    res = rule.find_contradictions(facts)
    assert len(res) == 1
    hint = res[0].resolution_hint or ""
    assert "обратный" in hint.lower() or "NAT" in hint

"""Тесты VirusTotalEnricher. HTTP замокан."""
from paradox.integrations.virustotal import VirusTotalEnricher


def test_vt_disabled_without_key(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("VIRUSTOTAL_API_KEY", raising=False)
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key=None)
    assert not e.enabled
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "not set" in result.error


def test_vt_enabled_with_key():
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    assert e.enabled


def test_vt_lookup_ip_success(monkeypatch):
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ioc, t: (
        {"data": {"attributes": {"last_analysis_stats": {"malicious": 0}}}},
        None,
    ))
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert result.ok
    assert result.source == "virustotal"
    assert "data" in result.data


def test_vt_lookup_domain_supported(monkeypatch):
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ioc, t: ({}, None))
    result = e.lookup("evil.com", "domain")
    assert result.ok


def test_vt_lookup_hash_supported(monkeypatch):
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ioc, t: ({}, None))
    result = e.lookup("a" * 64, "hash")
    assert result.ok

    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    result = e.lookup("1.2.3.4:80", "url")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "unsupported" in result.error


def test_vt_lookup_rate_limited(monkeypatch):
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ioc, t: (None, "rate limited (429)"))
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "429" in result.error


def test_vt_lookup_unauthorized(monkeypatch):
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="bad")
    monkeypatch.setattr(e, "_fetch", lambda ioc, t: (None, "unauthorized (401)"))
    result = e.lookup("8.8.8.8", "ip")
    assert not result.ok
    assert "401" in result.error


def test_vt_ttl_short():
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    assert e.ttl_days == 1


def test_vt_supports_ip_domain_hash():
    e = VirusTotalEnricher(api_key="fake")
    assert set(e.supports) == {"ip", "domain", "hash"}


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_x509_cert.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты парсера X.509 Certificate на синтетических мини-сертификатах."""
import pytest

from paradox.x509_der import DerError
from paradox.x509_cert import parse_certificate


# === Хелперы для сборки TLV ===

def _tlv(tag: int, value: bytes) -> bytes:
    n = len(value)
    if n < 0x80:
        return bytes([tag, n]) + value
    if n < 0x100:
        return bytes([tag, 0x81, n]) + value
    return bytes([tag, 0x82, (n >> 8) & 0xFF, n & 0xFF]) + value


def _seq(*parts: bytes) -> bytes:
    return _tlv(0x30, b"".join(parts))


def _set(*parts: bytes) -> bytes:
    return _tlv(0x31, b"".join(parts))


def _int(n: int) -> bytes:
    if n == 0:
        return bytes([0x02, 0x01, 0x00])
    length = (n.bit_length() + 7) // 8
    return bytes([0x02, length]) + n.to_bytes(length, "big")


def _null() -> bytes:
    return bytes([0x05, 0x00])


def _oid(arcs_str: str) -> bytes:
    parts = [int(x) for x in arcs_str.split(".")]
    first = parts[0] * 40 + parts[1]
    data = bytes([first])
    for arc in parts[2:]:
        if arc < 0x80:
            data += bytes([arc])
        else:
            buf = []
            while arc > 0x7F:
                buf.append((arc & 0x7F) | 0x80)
                arc >>= 7
            buf.append(arc)
            data += bytes(reversed(buf))
    return bytes([0x06, len(data)]) + data


def _printable(s: str) -> bytes:
    b = s.encode("ascii")
    return bytes([0x13, len(b)]) + b


def _utctime(s: str) -> bytes:
    b = s.encode("ascii")
    return bytes([0x17, len(b)]) + b


def _octet(b: bytes) -> bytes:
    return bytes([0x04, len(b)]) + b


def _ctx(num: int, value: bytes, constructed: bool = False) -> bytes:
    tag = 0x80 | (num & 0x1F)
    if constructed:
        tag |= 0x20
    return _tlv(tag, value)


def _rdn(cn: str) -> bytes:
    return _set(_seq(_oid("2.5.4.3"), _printable(cn)))


def _name(cn: str) -> bytes:
    return _seq(_rdn(cn))


def _validity(nb: str, na: str) -> bytes:
    return _seq(_utctime(nb), _utctime(na))


def _san_ext(dns: tuple, ips: tuple) -> bytes:
    """Extensions [3] c одним SAN."""
    gn = b""
    for d in dns:
        gn += _ctx(2, d.encode("ascii"))
    for ip in ips:
        gn += _ctx(7, ip)
    san_inner = _seq(gn)
    ext = _seq(_oid("2.5.29.17"), _octet(san_inner))
    return _seq(ext)  # SEQUENCE OF Extension


def _cert(
    subject_cn: str,
    issuer_cn: str,
    nb: str = "240101000000Z",
    na: str = "251231235959Z",
    san_dns: tuple = (),
    san_ips: tuple = (),
    include_version: bool = True,
) -> bytes:
    parts = []
    if include_version:
        parts.append(_ctx(0, _int(2), constructed=True))
    parts.append(_int(12345))                                        # serial
    parts.append(_seq(_oid("1.2.840.113549.1.1.11"), _null()))       # sigAlg
    parts.append(_name(issuer_cn))                                   # issuer
    parts.append(_validity(nb, na))                                  # validity
    parts.append(_name(subject_cn))                                  # subject
    parts.append(_seq(                                                # SPKI-заглушка
        _seq(_oid("1.2.840.113549.1.1.1"), _null()),
        _tlv(0x03, b"\x00" + b"\x00" * 4),
    ))
    if san_dns or san_ips:
        parts.append(_ctx(3, _san_ext(san_dns, san_ips), constructed=True))

    tbs = _seq(*parts)
    return _seq(
        tbs,
        _seq(_oid("1.2.840.113549.1.1.11"), _null()),
        _tlv(0x03, b"\x00" + b"\x00" * 8),
    )


# === Тесты ===

def test_parse_simple_v3():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.subject_cn == "example.com"
    assert cert.issuer_cn == "Test CA"
    assert cert.is_self_signed is False
    assert cert.not_before == "2024-01-01T00:00:00"
    assert cert.not_after == "2025-12-31T23:59:59"
    assert cert.san_dns == []
    assert cert.san_ips == []


def test_v1_cert_without_version():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA", include_version=False)
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.subject_cn == "example.com"
    assert cert.issuer_cn == "Test CA"


def test_self_signed_detected():
    der = _cert("example.com", "example.com")
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.is_self_signed is True


def test_self_signed_false():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.is_self_signed is False


def test_san_dns_only():
    der = _cert(
        "example.com", "Test CA",
        san_dns=("example.com", "www.example.com"),
    )
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.san_dns == ["example.com", "www.example.com"]
    assert cert.san_ips == []


def test_san_ips_only():
    der = _cert(
        "example.com", "Test CA",
        san_ips=(b"\x01\x02\x03\x04", b"\x0A\x00\x00\x01"),
    )
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.san_ips == ["1.2.3.4", "10.0.0.1"]


def test_san_mixed():
    der = _cert(
        "example.com", "Test CA",
        san_dns=("a.example",),
        san_ips=(b"\x7F\x00\x00\x01",),
    )
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.san_dns == ["a.example"]
    assert cert.san_ips == ["127.0.0.1"]


def test_no_san_no_extensions():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    cert = parse_certificate(der)
    assert cert.san_dns == []
    assert cert.san_ips == []


def test_broken_der_raises():
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_certificate(b"\x00\x01\x02")


def test_empty_der_raises():
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_certificate(b"")


def test_truncated_certificate_raises():
    der = _cert("example.com", "Test CA")
    # Обрезаем — length не сойдётся
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_certificate(der[:-20])


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_x509_der.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты минимального DER-парсера."""
import pytest

from paradox.x509_der import (
    DerError,
    TAG_INTEGER,
    TAG_OID,
    TAG_SEQUENCE,
    TAG_UTF8_STRING,
    as_int,
    as_oid,
    as_str,
    as_time,
    parse_der,
)


# === Базовый разбор ===

def test_parse_simple_sequence():
    # SEQUENCE { INTEGER 5 }
    # 30 03 02 01 05
    data = bytes.fromhex("3003020105")
    node = parse_der(data)
    assert node.tag == TAG_SEQUENCE
    assert node.constructed is True
    assert node.tag_class == 0
    assert len(node.children) == 1
    assert node.children[0].tag == TAG_INTEGER
    assert node.children[0].value == b"\x05"


def test_parse_empty_sequence():
    # SEQUENCE {} = 30 00
    data = bytes.fromhex("3000")
    node = parse_der(data)
    assert node.tag == TAG_SEQUENCE
    assert node.children == []
    assert node.value == b""


def test_parse_nested_sequence():
    # SEQUENCE { SEQUENCE { INTEGER 1 } INTEGER 2 }
    # 30 07  30 03 02 01 01  02 01 02
    data = bytes.fromhex("30083003020101020102")
    node = parse_der(data)
    assert node.tag == TAG_SEQUENCE
    assert len(node.children) == 2
    assert node.children[0].tag == TAG_SEQUENCE
    assert node.children[1].tag == TAG_INTEGER


def test_parse_long_form_length():
    # SEQUENCE длиной 130 байт (long form: 81 82)
    # value = 130 байт нулей
    value = b"\x00" * 130
    data = b"\x30\x81\x82" + value
    node = parse_der(data)
    assert node.tag == TAG_SEQUENCE
    assert len(node.value) == 130


def test_parse_long_form_2byte():
    # SEQUENCE длиной 500 (82 01 F4)
    value = b"\x00" * 500
    data = b"\x30\x82\x01\xF4" + value
    node = parse_der(data)
    assert len(node.value) == 500


# === INTEGER ===

def test_as_int_positive():
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("020105"))
    assert as_int(node) == 5


def test_as_int_zero():
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("020100"))
    assert as_int(node) == 0


def test_as_int_large():
    # INTEGER 256 = 02 02 01 00
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("02020100"))
    assert as_int(node) == 256


def test_as_int_negative():
    # INTEGER -1 = 02 01 FF (two's complement)
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("0201FF"))
    assert as_int(node) == -1


def test_as_int_wrong_tag():
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("06012A"))
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        as_int(node)


# === OID ===

def test_as_oid_common():
    # OID 2.5.29.17 (subjectAltName) = 06 03 55 1D 11
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("0603551D11"))
    assert as_oid(node) == "2.5.29.17"


def test_as_oid_short():
    # OID 1.2.840.113549.1.1.11 (sha256WithRSA)
    # 06 09 2A 86 48 86 F7 0D 01 01 0B
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("06092A864886F70D01010B"))
    assert as_oid(node) == "1.2.840.113549.1.1.11"




def test_as_oid_empty_raises():
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("0600"))
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        as_oid(node)


# === Строки ===

def test_as_str_utf8():
    # UTF8String "hi" = 0C 02 68 69
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("0C026869"))
    assert as_str(node) == "hi"


def test_as_str_printable():
    # PrintableString "CN" = 13 02 43 4E
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("1302434E"))
    assert as_str(node) == "CN"


def test_as_str_ia5():
    # IA5String "a@b" = 16 03 61 40 62
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("1603614062"))
    assert as_str(node) == "a@b"


def test_as_str_wrong_tag():
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("020105"))
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        as_str(node)


# === Время ===

def test_as_time_utc():
    # UTCTime "240101120000Z" = 17 0D 32 34 30 31 30 31 31 32 30 30 30 30 5A
    hex_str = "170D32343031303131323 03030305A".replace(" ", "")
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex(hex_str))
    result = as_time(node)
    assert result is not None
    iso, kind = result
    assert kind == "utc"
    assert iso.startswith("2024-01-01T12:00:00")


def test_as_time_utc_1950s():
    # UTCTime "500101000000Z" -> 1950-01-01
    hex_str = "170D3530303130313030303030305A"
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex(hex_str))
    result = as_time(node)
    assert result is not None
    iso, _ = result
    assert iso.startswith("1950-01-01")


def test_as_time_generalized():
    # GeneralizedTime "20240101120000Z" (15 символов + Z = 15)
    # 18 0F 32 30 32 34 30 31 30 31 31 32 30 30 30 30 5A
    hex_str = "180F32303234303130313132303030305A"
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex(hex_str))
    result = as_time(node)
    assert result is not None
    iso, kind = result
    assert kind == "generalized"
    assert iso.startswith("2024-01-01T12:00:00")


def test_as_time_wrong_tag():
    node = parse_der(bytes.fromhex("020105"))
    assert as_time(node) is None


# === Ошибки ===

def test_truncated_value_raises():
    # SEQUENCE заявляет 10 байт, реально 3
    data = bytes.fromhex("300A0201 05".replace(" ", ""))
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_der(data)


def test_indefinite_length_raises():
    # 30 80 ... — indefinite, запрещён в DER
    data = bytes.fromhex("3080020105")
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_der(data)


def test_empty_data_raises():
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_der(b"")


def test_high_tag_raises():
    # tag 0x1F — high-tag-number form
    data = bytes.fromhex("1F0105")
    with pytest.raises(DerError):
        parse_der(data)


# ==========================================
# FILE: tests/test_yara_detector.py
# ==========================================
"""Тесты YARA-детектора.

Ключевой момент: не полагаемся на наличие yara-python.
Если его нет — проверяем graceful degradation через mock.
Если есть — проверяем реальный скан с корректным MockCarvedFile.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from paradox.detectors.yara_detector import YARADetector
from paradox.models import CaptureState


# =============================================================================
# ОБЩИЕ ФИКСТУРЫ
# =============================================================================

@dataclass
class MockCarvedFile:
    """Минимальный слепок CarvedFile — только поля, которые читает yara_detector."""
    sha256: str = "abcdef1234567890" * 4
    uri: str = "/download/test.exe"
    saved_path: str | None = None
    first_bytes_hex: str = ""
    ext: str = ".exe"
    size: int = 100
    content_type: str | None = None
    src_ip: str = "10.0.0.5"
    dst_ip: str = "10.0.0.1"
    truncated: bool = False
    first_ts: float | None = None


@dataclass
class MockConfig:
    yara_rules_path: str | None = None


def _has_yara() -> bool:
    try:
        import yara  # noqa: F401
        return True
    except ImportError:
        return False


HAS_YARA = _has_yara()


# =============================================================================
# 1. GRACEFUL DEGRADATION — не падаем, если yara нет
# =============================================================================

def test_graceful_degradation_yara_not_installed():
    """Если yara-python не установлена — возвращаем [] без ошибок."""
    with patch("paradox.detectors.yara_detector.yara", None):
        detector = YARADetector()
        state = CaptureState()
        state.carved_files = [MockCarvedFile(first_bytes_hex="4d5a")]
        config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path="whatever.yar")

        findings = detector.run(state, config)
        assert findings == []


def test_no_rules_path_and_no_default():
    """Если не задан yara_rules_path и дефолтный файл не найден — возвращаем []."""
    if not HAS_YARA:
        pytest.skip("yara-python not installed")

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [MockCarvedFile(first_bytes_hex="4d5a")]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path=None)

    # Мокаем os.path.exists так, чтобы дефолтный rules/default.yar "не нашёлся"
    with patch("paradox.detectors.yara_detector.os.path.exists", return_value=False):
        findings = detector.run(state, config)
        assert findings == []


def test_broken_yara_rules_file(tmp_path):
    """Битый .yar-файл → ловим ошибку компиляции, возвращаем []."""
    if not HAS_YARA:
        pytest.skip("yara-python not installed")

    bad_rule = tmp_path / "broken.yar"
    bad_rule.write_text("rule Broken { strings: $a = condition: }", encoding="utf-8")

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [MockCarvedFile(first_bytes_hex="4d5a")]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path=str(bad_rule))

    findings = detector.run(state, config)
    assert findings == []


# =============================================================================
# 2. РЕАЛЬНЫЙ СКАН (если yara установлена)
# =============================================================================

@pytest.mark.skipif(not HAS_YARA, reason="yara-python not installed")
def test_yara_real_scan_saved_path(tmp_path):
    """Скан файла на диске через saved_path."""
    import yara  # noqa: F401

    rule_file = tmp_path / "malware.yar"
    rule_file.write_text(
        'rule MalwareSignature {\n'
        '    strings: $a = "EVIL_PAYLOAD"\n'
        '    condition: $a\n'
        '}',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    target = tmp_path / "evil.bin"
    target.write_bytes(b"some prefix EVIL_PAYLOAD some suffix")

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [
        MockCarvedFile(saved_path=str(target), first_bytes_hex=""),
    ]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path=str(rule_file))

    findings = detector.run(state, config)
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].type == "yara_match"
    assert findings[0].value == "MalwareSignature"
    assert findings[0].severity == "high"
    assert findings[0].evidence["rule"] == "MalwareSignature"


@pytest.mark.skipif(not HAS_YARA, reason="yara-python not installed")
def test_yara_real_scan_first_bytes_fallback(tmp_path):
    """Fallback на first_bytes_hex, когда saved_path=None."""
    import yara  # noqa: F401

    rule_file = tmp_path / "malware.yar"
    rule_file.write_text(
        'rule MZHeader {\n'
        '    strings: $a = { 4D 5A }\n'  # MZ — сигнатура exe
        '    condition: $a\n'
        '}',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [
        # "4d5a" — hex от b"MZ", первые байты PE-файла
        MockCarvedFile(saved_path=None, first_bytes_hex="4d5a9090"),
    ]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path=str(rule_file))

    findings = detector.run(state, config)
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].value == "MZHeader"


@pytest.mark.skipif(not HAS_YARA, reason="yara-python not installed")
def test_yara_invalid_hex_does_not_crash(tmp_path):
    """Если first_bytes_hex битый — не падаем."""
    import yara  # noqa: F401

    rule_file = tmp_path / "any.yar"
    rule_file.write_text(
        'rule Any { strings: $a = "x" condition: $a }',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [
        MockCarvedFile(saved_path=None, first_bytes_hex="невалидный hex"),
    ]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path=str(rule_file))

    # Не падаем, ловим warning через logger
    findings = detector.run(state, config)
    assert findings == []


@pytest.mark.skipif(not HAS_YARA, reason="yara-python not installed")
def test_yara_no_duplicate_findings(tmp_path):
    """Один и тот же файл не должен давать два finding (saved_path + first_bytes)."""
    import yara  # noqa: F401

    rule_file = tmp_path / "dup.yar"
    rule_file.write_text(
        'rule Duplicate { strings: $a = "MARKER" condition: $a }',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    target = tmp_path / "dup.bin"
    target.write_bytes(b"MARKER_MARKER")
    mz_hex = target.read_bytes()[:64].hex()

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [
        MockCarvedFile(saved_path=str(target), first_bytes_hex=mz_hex),
    ]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path=str(rule_file))

    findings = detector.run(state, config)
    # Правило одно, файл один → один finding, несмотря на два прогона сканера
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert findings[0].value == "Duplicate"


# =============================================================================
# 3. МОК-ТЕСТ: сканер вызван, но ничего не нашёл
# =============================================================================

@patch("paradox.detectors.yara_detector.yara")
def test_yara_no_matches(mock_yara):
    """Сканер отработал, но совпадений нет → findings пустой."""
    mock_rules = MagicMock()
    mock_rules.match.return_value = []
    mock_yara.compile.return_value = mock_rules

    detector = YARADetector()
    state = CaptureState()
    state.carved_files = [MockCarvedFile(first_bytes_hex="4d5a")]
    config = MockConfig(yara_rules_path="dummy.yar")

    # os.path.exists должен вернуть True для dummy.yar
    with patch("paradox.detectors.yara_detector.os.path.exists", return_value=True):
        findings = detector.run(state, config)

    assert findings == []
    assert mock_yara.compile.called
